TL;DR: 该研究探讨了视觉语言模型中的视觉锁定现象，即过时的语言描述会误导模型对当前场景的判断。研究发现，锁定现象与模型表示变化的幅度无关，而与变化的组织方式有关。在难以纠正的模型中，先前信息引起的改变集中在一组重复出现的特定方向上，称为‘先验方向’（Prior Directions）。通过控制干预实验发现，移除与这些方向对齐的成分可恢复视觉定位能力，而移除同等大小的正交成分影响甚微。这表明，先验控制源于先验引发的变化在决策表示中形成一致且可复用的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型依赖早期视觉状态的描述来做出当前决策，当场景发生变化时，过时的语言描述可能导致错误判断，这种现象被称为视觉锁定。研究旨在理解视觉锁定的机制及其在不同模型间的差异。

Method: 在受控的语义定位环境中，仅改变语言化之前的描述，分析不同模型在视觉表示上的变化。通过比较模型表示的变化方向，识别出反复出现的‘先验方向’，并设计控制干预实验验证其对视觉接地的影响。

Result: 研究发现，先验诱导的变化集中在少数特定方向（即先验方向），这些方向在不同示例中重复出现；移除与这些方向对齐的成分可显著恢复视觉接地能力，而移除正交成分则无明显效果。表明锁定效应与变化的结构性组织相关，而非变化量的大小。

Conclusion: 视觉锁定的发生并非由表示变化的绝对大小决定，而是由先验引发的变化是否形成一致、可复用的方向性模式所决定。这一机制解释了为何同一先验在某些模型中可被修正，而在另一些模型中却占据主导地位。

Abstract: Vision-language models often use descriptions of earlier visual states to make decisions about the current scene. When the scene changes, stale language can redirect an otherwise correct visual judgment toward an outdated answer. We study this failure as visual lock-in in a controlled grounding setting where only the verbalized prior varies. Across models, stronger lock-in accompanies smaller changes in the model representation before the final answer. This reversal suggests that lock-in depends not on how far this representation moves, but on how that movement is organized. In models that are harder to correct, prior-induced changes concentrate along a compact set of directions that repeatedly appear across examples. We call these recurrent axes the Prior Directions. They recur on held-out examples, while a descriptive four-model comparison associates greater concentration with stronger lock-in. Controlled interventions show that removing the component aligned with the Prior Directions restores visual grounding, whereas removing an equally large orthogonal component has little effect. Prior control thus arises when prior-induced changes form a coherent and reusable pattern in the representation used to produce the answer. This account explains why the same prior remains revisable in one model yet becomes dominant in another.

</details>


### [67] [From Keypoints to Predictive Distributions: Post-Hoc Uncertainty for YOLO-Pose Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26921)
*Alexej Klushyn,Juan Rivero Sesma,Florian Seligmann,Richard Kurle,Kinh Tieu,Jayant Sen Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级后处理概率扩展，为YOLO-Pose模型添加校准的双变量预测分布，以量化关键点位置的空间不确定性。通过训练额外的概率头来预测每个关键点的输入相关2×2分散矩阵，并采用高斯或学生-t校准方法以保证下游兼容性或分布保真度。引入了一种结合分布校准诊断与关键点精度（AKP）的新评估协议，实验表明所学不确定性估计可有效进行关键点可靠性排序，学生-t校准最能捕捉实际残差分布，且基于不确定性的剪枝可移除不可靠关键点。在视觉引导飞机着陆的应用中，跑道关键点的校准协方差支持不确定性感知的位置估计和传感器融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YOLO-Pose模型虽高效定位关键点，但缺乏对空间不确定性的量化，限制了其在高可靠性要求场景中的应用。因此需要一种轻量级方法，在不改变原模型结构的前提下，为关键点预测提供可靠且可校准的不确定性估计。

Method: 在已训练的YOLO-Pose模型基础上，增加概率头部，利用重要性加权负对数似然损失训练，预测每个关键点的2×2分散矩阵；随后采用高斯或学生-t校准方法，使预测分布更贴近真实误差分布；同时设计包含分布校准诊断与关键点级精度（AKP）的评估协议。

Result: 实验在COCO数据集上验证了该方法的有效性：学习到的不确定性可实现关键点级别的可靠性排序；学生-t校准最准确地拟合实际残差分布；基于不确定性的剪枝策略能有效剔除不可靠关键点；在飞机着陆任务中，校准后的协方差支持了更可靠的位姿估计与传感器融合。

Conclusion: 本方法成功为YOLO-Pose模型引入了轻量、可校准的关键点不确定性估计，显著提升了关键点预测的可靠性与实用性，尤其适用于对安全性要求高的视觉系统应用场景。

Abstract: YOLO-Pose models provide efficient keypoint localization, but do not quantify the associated spatial uncertainty. We introduce a lightweight post-hoc probabilistic extension that augments a trained YOLO-Pose model with calibrated bivariate predictive distributions over keypoint locations, centered at the model's original predictions. Concretely, we train additional probabilistic heads with an importance-weighted negative log-likelihood to predict an input-dependent $2\times2$ dispersion matrix for each keypoint, followed by Gaussian calibration for broad downstream compatibility or Student-$t$ calibration for distributional fidelity. Complementing this, we propose an evaluation protocol that combines a suite of distributional calibration diagnostics with average keypoint precision (AKP), a keypoint-level extension of the COCO AP protocol for assessing reliability rankings. Experiments on COCO show that the learned uncertainty estimates enable effective keypoint-level reliability ranking, Student-$t$ calibration best captures the empirical residual distribution, and uncertainty-based pruning removes unreliable keypoints. A central application-level demonstration is vision-based aircraft landing, where calibrated covariances for runway keypoints support uncertainty-aware aircraft position estimation and downstream sensor fusion.

</details>


### [68] [Progressive Multimodal Alignment for Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26947)
*Duzhen Zhang,Yahan Yu,Qiaoyi Su,Jiahua Dong,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PMA框架以解决多模态持续指令微调中投影器因分布漂移导致的遗忘问题，通过轻量级表示描述符检测分布变化，仅在需要时逐步扩展投影专家，并利用可扩展路由器融合专家输出，同时保留预训练投影器作为稳定对齐锚点，实现稳定与可塑性的平衡，参数增长接近线性，且可适配多种MLLM骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注LLM主干的持续学习，忽视了多模态持续指令微调中由于视觉分布变化和指令语义演化导致的投影器漂移和投影器级遗忘问题。

Method: PMA通过轻量级表示描述符检测多模态分布变化，仅在必要时逐步扩展投影专家；使用可扩展路由器根据多模态特征融合专家输出，同时保留原始预训练投影器作为稳定对齐锚点，实现渐进式适应与知识保留。

Result: 在两个最新的MCIT基准上实验表明，结合PMA能显著提升性能，优于现有最先进方法；PMA具有良好的可扩展性，适用于多种多模态大模型骨干网络，展现出稳健且普适的持续学习效果。

Conclusion: PMA有效缓解了投影器级遗忘问题，实现了多模态持续学习中的稳定性与可塑性平衡，是一种高效、通用且可插拔的改进方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) rely on a projector to align visual representations with the language embedding space, making it central to cross-modal understanding. In Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT), however, shifting visual distributions and evolving instruction semantics cause this shared projector to drift, leading to projector-level forgetting, an issue largely overlooked by methods that focus primarily on the LLM backbone. We introduce Progressive Multimodal Alignment (PMA), a framework that enables the projector to adapt continually while preserving previously learned alignment. PMA detects multimodal distribution shifts via a lightweight representation descriptor and progressively expands projector experts only when needed. An expandable router integrates expert outputs based on multimodal features, while the original pretrained projector is retained as a stable alignment anchor. This progressive mechanism balances stability and plasticity with sub-linear parameter growth and serves as a method-agnostic add-on to existing MCIT approaches. Extensive experiments on two recent MCIT benchmarks demonstrate that mitigating projector-level forgetting yields consistent gains over prior state-of-the-art methods when combined with PMA. Moreover, PMA scales across diverse MLLM backbones, demonstrating robust and broadly applicable MCIT performance.

</details>


### [69] [Robust RPC Bundle Adjustment for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery with Season-Invariant Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26973)
*Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Xavier Bou,Thibaud Ehret,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种外观感知的RPC精化流水线，结合学习到的局部特征匹配与全局图像描述符，以实现季节不变的对应关系和视觉兼容的图像对选择，从而在多日期卫星影像中提升RPC精化精度并减少匹配时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RPC捆绑调整依赖手工特征匹配，在受季节、光照和地表覆盖变化影响的多日期影像集合中表现不可靠。需要一种更鲁棒的方法来应对这些外观变化。

Method: 采用学习的局部特征匹配以获取季节不变的对应关系，并结合全局图像描述符筛选视觉上兼容的图像对，从而减少冗余和错误匹配，同时保持匹配图的连通性。

Result: 在季节差异明显的WorldView-3影像数据集上，相比开源基线方法，该方法在无需地面控制点的情况下显著降低了几何一致性误差，并大幅减少了39-42视图集合的匹配时间。

Conclusion: 该方法提升了RPC精化对时序外观变化的鲁棒性，使多日期卫星影像得以更有效地利用。

Abstract: Accurate refinement of Rational Polynomial Camera (RPC) models is essential for high-quality satellite image geolocation. In ground control point (GCP)-free multi-view pipelines, this refinement is commonly performed through bundle adjustment from automatically extracted image correspondences. However, conventional RPC bundle adjustment pipelines rely on handcrafted feature matching, which becomes unreliable in multi-date collections affected by seasonal, illumination, and land-cover changes. We propose an appearance-aware RPC refinement pipeline that combines learned local feature matching for season-invariant correspondences with global image descriptors for selecting visually compatible image pairs. This reduces redundant and error-prone matching while preserving the connectivity of the matching graph. Experiments on seasonally diverse WorldView-3 images show that our pipeline improves GCP-free relative RPC refinement over open-source baselines, achieving lower geometric consistency errors while substantially reducing matching time on collections with 39-42 views. By making RPC refinement more robust to diachronic appearance variation, our approach enables more effective use of multi-date satellite imagery.

</details>


### [70] [Mitigating Compounding Error via Video Representation Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27036)
*Taiye Chen,Qi Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视频扩散世界模型中的自回归误差累积问题，发现误差累积与隐藏表示的维度坍缩密切相关。通过引入有效秩（erank）作为量化指标，揭示了表示退化与长期生成不稳定性之间的强关联。尽管数据规模扩展无法提升模型抗漂移能力，但提出的视频表示正则化方法显著提升了长时视频生成质量，在VBench的美学质量和成像质量上分别从38.65/44.37提升至55.56/72.08，为稳定长视频生成提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在长时间自回归生成中存在严重的误差累积问题，导致帧质量随时间下降，但其内在机制尚不明确，且主流的数据扩展策略对此无效，亟需新的理论理解与解决方案。

Method: 提出视频表示正则化方法，通过轻量级训练约束稳定潜在表示，抑制迭代误差累积；同时采用有效秩（erank）作为量化误差积累的指标，分析内部表示动态。

Result: 在VBench评测中，美学质量从38.65提升至55.56，成像质量从44.37提升至72.08，显著优于Diffusion Forcing；验证了表示退化与生成漂移的强关联，并揭示了数据扩展的局限性。

Conclusion: 首次建立了自回归视频漂移与模型内部表示之间的联系，证明维度坍缩是误差累积的关键原因，提出有效的正则化策略，为实现鲁棒的长时视频生成提供新范式。

Abstract: Video diffusion-based world models enable long autoregressive video generation for robotics, autonomous driving and simulation tasks, yet sliding-window autoregressive inference suffers from severe error accumulation that degrades frame quality over time. Although this phenomenon has been widely observed, the underlying mechanism of compounding error and how to achieve stable long-horizon generation remain largely unresolved. In this paper, we investigate the internal representation dynamics of video world models and discover that compounding error is tightly coupled with dimensional collapse of hidden representations. Specifically, the effective rank of model representations sharply decreases at the onset of generation drift, revealing a strong connection between representational degradation and long-term rollout instability. Furthermore, we find that pure training data scaling fails to boost model resistance to error drift, contradicting mainstream scaling paradigms. To address this problem, we propose video representation regularization, a lightweight training constraint that stabilizes latent representations and suppresses iterative error accumulation. Compared with Diffusion Forcing, our method achieves improvements from 38.65 to 55.56 and from 44.37 to 72.08 on the Aesthetic Quality and Imaging Quality metrics of VBench. Our work establishes the first connection between autoregressive video drifting and model internal representations, adopts erank as a quantitative metric for error accumulation, reveals counterintuitive scaling limitations for video world models, and presents a simple yet effective regularization strategy to improve long video generation robustness.

</details>


### [71] [Object Detection for Autonomous Driving in Chinese Rural Scenes: An Experimental Study on Real-Synthetic Data Mixing and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27058)
*Danning Zhu,Ziyan Lin,Jing Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个专为中文农村道路设计的真-虚拟混合目标检测数据集，结合河南卫辉县的真实图像与Unreal Engine生成的虚拟场景，构建包含14类区域特有物体（如电动三轮车、低速车、路边摊等）的标注体系。在统一训练协议下，系统评估了13种主流检测器（包括YOLOv5/v8/v11/26系列及RT-DETR-L）在三种数据配比（全真实、1:0.5、1:1真实-虚拟）下的性能。结果表明，适度引入合成数据（1:0.5）可显著提升检测效果，其中YOLO11m达到最高mAP@0.5为0.758；但过高的合成数据比例（1:1）引发域偏移，抵消数据量优势。多数模型对常见车辆识别可靠，但对长尾、非标准物体（如摊位、护栏）仍存在感知瓶颈。研究为农村自动驾驶感知系统的模型选型与数据策略提供了实证支持和新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶目标检测模型在复杂中国农村交通场景中面临数据稀缺与泛化能力不足的挑战，尤其缺乏针对区域特有交通元素（如电动三轮车、低速车、路边摊等）的高质量标注数据，制约了模型在真实场景中的部署效果。

Method: 构建一个融合真实图像（来自河南卫辉县）与参数化虚拟场景（通过Unreal Engine生成）的混合数据集，定义涵盖14类农村特有物体的标注体系，并在统一训练框架下，对13种主流检测器在三种不同真实-虚拟数据比例（1:0, 1:0.5, 1:1）下进行系统性评估，以分析合成数据对模型性能的影响。

Result: 适度注入合成数据（1:0.5比例）能有效提升检测性能，其中YOLO11m在该配置下取得最高mAP@0.5为0.758；但当合成数据占比过高（1:1）时，因域偏移导致性能下降；多数模型对典型车辆识别良好，但对长尾、非标准对象（如摊位、护栏）仍存在显著感知缺陷。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建专用混合数据集并系统评估不同数据策略，验证了合理使用合成数据对提升农村自动驾驶感知模型泛化能力的有效性，同时揭示了高比例合成数据带来的域偏移风险，为农村地区自动驾驶系统的设计与优化提供了关键实证依据与实践指导。

Abstract: Currently, autonomous driving object detection models face significant data scarcity and generalization challenges when navigating complex Chinese rural traffic scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a novel real-synthetic mixed object detection dataset tailored specifically for Chinese rural roads and systematically evaluate the performance of 13 mainstream detectors under different real-to-synthetic data ratios, thereby providing empirical evidence for model selection and data strategy design in rural autonomous driving scenarios. Our dataset combines real-world images captured in Weishi County, Henan Province, with parameterized virtual scenes generated via Unreal Engine. To accurately reflect the unique realities of rural traffic, we define a comprehensive 14-category object system encompassing region-specific elements such as electric tricycles, low-speed vehicles (LSVs), and roadside stalls. Under a unified training protocol, we systematically evaluate 13 mainstream detectors -- spanning the YOLOv5, YOLOv8, YOLO11, and YOLO26 series, as well as RT-DETR-L -- across three data configurations: an all-real baseline, a 1:0.5 real-to-virtual mix, and a 1:1 mix. Experimental results demonstrate that a moderate injection of synthetic data (1:0.5 ratio) effectively enhances detection performance, with YOLO11m achieving the highest mAP@0.5 of 0.758. However, a higher proportion of synthetic data (1:1) introduces domain shifts that offset the benefits of data scaling. While most models reliably identify distinct local vehicles, significant perceptual bottlenecks remain for long-tail, non-standard objects like stalls and railings. This research provides crucial empirical evidence and novel insights for model selection and synthetic data strategies, facilitating the practical deployment of autonomous driving perception systems in rural areas.

</details>


### [72] [ScratchSim: A Procedural Synthetic Data Pipeline for Surface Scratch Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27065)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Tiago Kleist,Richard Hoffmann,Arjan kuijper,Michael Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于BlenderProc的程序化渲染流水线，用于生成大规模带标注的合成训练数据，支持可配置材质、相机模式和领域随机化，并自动输出COCO格式标注。通过在两种不同材质物体上评估四种训练策略（纯合成、纯真实、混合、从合成权重微调），结果表明：从合成权重微调性能优于纯真实训练，混合训练在真实数据稀缺时能有效恢复性能，且在卷积与基于Transformer的检测器上均验证有效。该方法使缺陷检测无需依赖大量真实标注数据，适用于边缘部署的工业质检。项目代码、3D模型及合成与真实标注数据集将公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业质量控制中表面划痕等缺陷检测面临标注缺陷数据稀缺的挑战，真实标注数据获取成本高，限制了深度学习模型的训练效果。

Method: 采用BlenderProc构建程序化渲染流水线，生成具有可配置材质、相机参数和领域随机化的合成图像，并自动生成COCO格式标注。通过对比合成仅训练、真实仅训练、混合训练及从合成权重微调等策略，评估多种轻量级检测器（YOLOX、YOLO26、LW-DETR）在不同条件下的表现。

Result: 从合成权重微调显著优于纯真实训练；混合训练在真实数据有限时能有效提升性能；该方法在多种架构上均表现稳健，支持轻量化模型在边缘设备上的应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的合成数据生成方法有效缓解了真实标注数据稀缺问题，为工业缺陷检测提供了可扩展、低成本的解决方案，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: While automated defect detection such as the detection of surface scratched is an important aspect in industrial quality control, the scarcity of annotated defect data make this task challenging. This paper presents a procedural rendering pipeline that generates large-scale annotated synthetic training data using BlenderProc, with configurable material appearance, camera modes, and domain randomization, producing automatic COCO-format annotations. To show the potential of our approach, we evaluate four training strategies, namely synthetic-only, real-only, mixed, and fine-tuning from synthetic weights, across two objects with different material properties and three lightweight edge-deployable detectors, YOLOX, YOLO26, and LW-DETR. Our evaluation show that fine-tuning from synthetic weights consistently outperforms real-only training, and that mixed training effectively recovers performance under scarce real-data conditions, with findings validated across both convolutional and transformer-based architectures. The proposed approach enables scalable defect detection without the burden of large real annotated datasets, making it practical for on-device industrial inspection. The pipeline scripts, 3D model, and both synthetic and real annotated scratch datasets for a glossy toy Ferrari car will be made available through the project website upon acceptance.

</details>


### [73] [SciFigAlign: Scoring Scientific Figures by Fine-tuned Alignment of Visuals with Manuscript Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27066)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Zihan Deng,Huiqi Liang,Chengkun Yue,Zhanlin Cui,Pengfei Ye,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于论文上下文的科学图表评估方法SciFigAlign，针对传统图像质量评估在科学图表评价中的局限性，构建了包含3,857个已发表会议论文图表的标注数据集，涵盖清晰度、相关性、信息量和结构四个评审维度。通过联合优化跨模态注意力与CubeMLP融合的多模态模型，结合平滑L1损失和论文内排序铰链损失，在测试集上实现0.3524的宏平均绝对误差（MAE）和81.64%的成对准确率，相比最佳基线降低59%相对误差。消融实验证明，论文上下文、引用段落去噪和排序监督对性能至关重要，表明科学图表评估需依赖视觉与文本证据的深层对齐，而非仅靠提示工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像质量评估方法无法有效判断科学图表是否支持论文论点；CLIP等通用图文匹配模型缺乏对论文上下文的理解；而零样本大模型作为评分器时存在评分集中、难以融合多源证据的问题。因此亟需一种能将图表质量评估与论文内容深度对齐的方法。

Method: 提出SciFigAlign模型，采用细调的CLIP与SciBERT，引入跨模态注意力机制和CubeMLP融合模块，以图块、标题、引用段落及轻量论文上下文为输入，联合优化平滑L1回归损失与论文内排序铰链损失，实现端到端的多模态对齐。

Result: 在论文级划分的测试集上，模型达到0.3524的宏平均绝对误差（MAE）和81.64%的成对准确率，相比最优基线（MAE 0.864）减少59%相对误差；消融实验验证了上下文输入、引用段落去噪和排序监督的关键作用。

Conclusion: 科学图表评估不能仅依赖通用图像或大模型评分，必须建立视觉内容与论文证据之间的深层对齐；SciFigAlign通过上下文感知的多模态建模，显著提升了评估准确性，为自动化同行评审中的图表质量判断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scientific figure assessment in peer review differs fundamentally from general image quality evaluation: a figure must be visually legible, faithfully support the manuscript's claims, and communicate evidence with a clear visual hierarchy. However, if we apply traditional image assessment methods to scientific figure quality assessment, limitations emerge: classic IQA models capture perceptual quality or aesthetics but cannot judge whether a figure serves the paper's scientific argument; CLIP-based methods assess generic image-text correspondence, yet lack understanding of manuscript context; and zero-shot LLM/VLM judges, when repurposed for figure scoring, often yield overly concentrated scores with limited fusion of visual and textual evidence. We introduce an annotated dataset of 3,857 scientific figures from peer-reviewed conference papers, each rated along four peer-review-oriented dimensions: Clarity, Relevance, Informativeness, and Structure. We propose SciFigAlign, a fine-tuned multimodal scorer that grounds figure quality assessment in manuscript evidence. Given a figure crop, caption, citing paragraphs, and light paper context, SciFigAlign fine-tunes CLIP and SciBERT end-to-end with per-modality cross-attention and CubeMLP fusion, jointly optimizing SmoothL1 regression with a within-paper ranking hinge loss. Under paper-level splits, SciFigAlign achieves a macro MAE of 0.3524 and a within-paper pairwise accuracy of 81.64% on the test set with n = 396, a 59% relative error reduction over the best LLM-as-judge baseline with MAE 0.864. Ablations confirm that manuscript-grounded inputs, citing-context denoising, and ranking supervision are all critical, showing that scientific figure assessment requires learned alignment between visual content and manuscript evidence rather than prompting alone, even with state-of-the-art VLMs.

</details>


### [74] [Visual Credit Audit for Multimodal Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27069)
*Feixiang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Lanbo Sun,Nan Wei,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VCA提出一种无需训练和标签的审计方法，用于评估视觉模型在空间推理任务中是否真正依赖图像信息。通过对比图像、文本和空白控制条件，分离出模型决策是否因图像而获得额外支持，以及是否响应特定关系的视觉证据。结果显示，12.73-26.25%的正确回答未被正确归因，且图像置换实验显著降低性能，表明多数正确答案依赖于图像。此外，模型对关系反转仍保持高响应率，说明其行为可能基于非语义线索。该方法可分解基准测试的成功为正确性、图像支持与关系一致性三部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准在封闭问答设置下可能奖励仅靠文本或无图像也能做出的正确答案，无法区分模型是否真正利用了图像中的视觉信息。因此需要一种方法来独立评估图像对模型决策的支持作用及对特定视觉关系的响应能力。

Method: 提出视觉信用审计（VCA），通过固定选择界面下的对比实验，分别评估：1）图像是否比文本或空白条件提供更多支持；2）模型是否响应特定关系的视觉证据。采用相同分割图像置换、固定像素关系对比和3×3证据源因子设计，结合标注数据进行依赖性归因的正确性分析（D-CC）和预测对齐扩展。

Result: 在四个开放多模态大模型和两个空间基准上，12.73%-26.25%的正确决策未被图像支持所归因；图像置换使D-CC下降21.25-47.80点，所有置信区间均高于零；模型对关系反转仍保持81.57%-100%的响应率，但整体答案改变仅32.11%；几何兼容编辑验证显示自然图像对应关系具有边界约束。

Conclusion: VCA成功将基准测试表现分解为三个维度：答案正确性、图像提供的额外支持、以及对视觉关系的一致性响应。这揭示了当前基准中大量正确但未被归因的决策，并强调必须使用严格控制的审计方法才能准确评估模型的视觉理解能力。

Abstract: Closed yes/no spatial benchmarks can reward a correct answer even when the image adds little support beyond no-image contexts. Under a fixed forced-choice interface, Visual Credit Audit (VCA) separates two estimands: whether the benchmark image gives the model's declared decision more support than text-only and blank controls, and whether the model responds to relation-specific visual evidence. The first audit is training- and label-free and does not require an answer flip. Applying labels yields dependence-credited correctness (D-CC); on correct items, it equals same-control gold-aligned positive gain, while prediction alignment extends the audit to errors. Across four open MLLMs and two spatial benchmarks, 12.73-26.25% of decisions are correct yet uncredited. Matched same-split image permutation reduces D-CC by 21.25-47.80 points, with every paired 95% interval above zero. Fixed-pixel relation contrasts and a 3x3 evidence-source factorial show why null controls cannot identify relation response. Among controlled correct-but-uncredited agreement decisions, response to relation reversal spans 81.57-100.00%, while 32.11% pooled change answer. Independently audited outcomes on 108 geometry-compatible edits provide a bounded natural-image correspondence check. VCA thereby decomposes benchmark success into correctness, additional image support, and relation-consistent response.

</details>


### [75] [SciFigQual-Bench: A Benchmark for Scientific Figure Quality Assessment with Full-Manuscript Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27084)
*Zihan Deng,Chuanzhi Xu,Huiqi Liang,Haoyang Li,Xiaozhen Zhong,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SciFigQual-Bench基准，用于评估科学图像在清晰度、布局、标题匹配度、上下文相关性和误导风险五个维度的表现。该基准结合图像、标题、引用句子和全文上下文，构建了6308张图像的专家标注数据集，并设计SFQ-Agent跨模态评估框架，实现可审计、精细化的评分。GPT-5.6-Sol驱动的SFQ-Agent在1200张测试图像上表现最佳，平均绝对误差最低（0.418），一致性最高（93.4%），优于直接评估与辅助模型方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法主要针对自然照片或AI生成内容，无法有效评估科学论文中的图表；已有研究仅关注视觉表面比较，忽略标题一致性、引用相关性及视觉误导等问题，亟需一个全面、上下文感知的科学图像评估框架。

Method: 构建包含图像、标题、引用句和全文上下文的多模态数据集，设计基于多阶段跨模态融合的SFQ-Agent评估框架，通过收集并整合不同模态证据实现自动化、可审计的评分。

Result: 在1200张测试图像上，SFQ-Agent（F3）搭配GPT-5.6-Sol达到最低平均绝对误差（0.418）和最高一致性（93.4%），显著优于直接评估和辅助模型方法，验证了其在科学图像评估中的有效性与优越性。

Conclusion: SciFigQual-Bench与SFQ-Agent共同构成首个面向科学图像的全文本上下文评估体系，为科学图像质量评价提供了标准化、可扩展的技术路径，推动科研可视化可信度提升。

Abstract: Scientific images are the core elements of presenting experimental conclusions, elaborating system architecture, and supporting comparative arguments in scientific papers. However, existing image quality assessment (IQA) methods are predominantly designed for natural photographs or AI-generated content, which cannot be directly applied to scientific papers. The few existing studies on scholarly charts remain confined to visual-surface comparisons, failing to verify caption alignment, citation relevance, or visual misleadingness. To address this, we propose SciFigQual-Bench, a full-text contextual benchmark that evaluates scientific images across five dimensions (clarity, layout, caption fit, context relevance, and misleading risk). The data covers top computer-science conferences from 2020 to 2025; 6,308 images were independently scored by multiple domain experts in five dimensions and aggregated into gold-standard annotations. Unlike previous scientific figure benchmarks, our dataset binds each image to its caption, citing sentence, and manuscript context. To enable automated evaluation on this benchmark, we designed a staged cross-modal evaluation framework SFQ-Agent to achieve auditable and refined scoring through the collection and fusion of modal evidence. Multiple mainstream large models were evaluated on the test subset eval1200, and SFQ-Agent (F3) equipped with GPT-5.6-Sol achieved the lowest overall average absolute error (0.418) and the highest consistency rate (93.4%), consistently outperforming both direct evaluation and auxiliary (Sidecar) visual language model evaluation schemes.

</details>


### [76] [Step-Attention Refinement of DINOv3 Features for Efficient Anterior Eye Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27087)
*Philippe Baumstimler,Jean-Mathieu Gagnon,Sébastien Gagné,Mathieu Duchesneau,Clément Playout,Lama Séoud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蒸馏DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络的轻量级架构，用于临床环境中前眼段（AES）的鲁棒分割。通过引入逐步注意力特征精炼模块，对多层级Transformer表示进行渐进式适应，再经卷积解码，高效利用预训练特征且参数极少。在包含333张临床采集图像的私有数据集上评估，涵盖八种眼科成像协议和七类解剖结构标注，相比卷积与Transformer基线方法（包括DINOv3基线），该方法在全微调下达到85.55% mIoU，表现最优；并在四个未见的公开AES分割数据集上展现出最强的域迁移鲁棒性。结果表明，该方法为临床场景下的鲁棒AES分割建立了强基准，并强调了解码器设计在有效适配基础模型表征至医学分割任务中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES分割方法受限于医疗环境下异构成像条件和标注稀缺，导致鲁棒性和泛化能力不足。尽管基础模型如DINOv3具备强大迁移能力，但如何高效将其表示适配至密集预测任务仍具挑战。因此，亟需一种轻量、高效且对域偏移鲁棒的分割框架，以满足临床实际需求。

Method: 提出基于蒸馏DINOv3 ViT-Small的轻量级分割架构，核心创新为步进式注意力特征精炼模块，用于在解码前逐层优化多尺度Transformer特征表示；结合卷积解码器，实现对预训练特征的高效利用，同时保持低参数量。

Result: 在私有临床数据集上，方法达到85.55% mIoU，优于各类卷积与变压器基线（含DINOv3基线）；在四个未见公共数据集上表现出最强的域迁移鲁棒性，验证了其泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一种高效、轻量且鲁棒的AES分割框架，证明了通过精心设计的解码器可有效适配基础模型表征至医学图像分割任务。该方法为临床环境下的前眼段分析提供了强有力的基准，凸显了解码器设计在模型迁移中的关键作用。

Abstract: Anterior eye segment (AES) segmentation is a key component of both ocular biometrics and emerging clinical image analysis applications. However, heterogeneous acquisition conditions and limited annotations in medical settings hinder the robustness and generalization of existing methods. Foundation models (FMs) such as DINOv3 offer strong transfer capabilities, but efficiently adapting their representations to dense prediction tasks remains challenging. In this study, we investigate robust AES segmentation in clinical settings, and propose a lightweight architecture built upon a distilled DINOv3 ViT-Small backbone. We introduce a step-attention feature refinement module that progressively adapts multi-level transformer representations before convolutional decoding, enabling efficient exploitation of pretrained features with few parameters. We evaluate the proposed approach on a private dataset of 333 clinically acquired AES images spanning eight ophthalmic acquisition protocols and annotated for seven anatomical classes. Compared with convolutional and transformer-based baselines, including DINOv3-based methods, our approach achieves the best overall performance, reaching 85.55\% mIoU when fully fine-tuned. It also demonstrates the strongest robustness to domain shift across four unseen public AES segmentation datasets. These results establish a strong baseline for robust AES segmentation in clinical settings and highlight the importance of decoder design for effectively adapting FMs representations to medical segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [77] [FreqForcing: Autoregressive Long Video Generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27110)
*Jiatong Li,Leo Liang,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqForcing框架，通过频域自锚定（SSA）解决长视频生成中因自回放导致的误差累积问题。该方法利用低频成分保持视觉稳定性，同时保留高频动态运动信息，在不需额外训练的情况下实现2分钟视频生成，相比现有无训练方法显著提升质量，并接近有训练方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长时间生成中因自回放误差累积导致颜色漂移、运动停滞和视觉崩溃，现有方法如注意力池化虽部分缓解但无法完全解决该问题。

Method: 提出频域自锚定（SSA），利用锚点注意力的低频成分维持长期视觉稳定，结合局部注意力的高频成分保留动态运动，构建无需训练的FreqForcing框架。

Result: FreqForcing将预训练的5秒模型扩展至2分钟生成，实现24倍外推；在定量与定性评估中均优于现有无训练方法，且与代表性有训练方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过频域分析揭示误差累积的本质为低频能量漂移，提出的FreqForcing框架有效缓解该问题，实现了高质量、长时序视频生成，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable real-time streaming video generation. However, errors introduced during self-rollout accumulate over long horizons, manifesting as color drift, motion stagnation, and eventual visual collapse. In this paper, we characterize this phenomenon from a frequency-domain perspective: error accumulation appears as a pronounced energy drift in the low-frequency bands. We further investigate the effectiveness of attention sink in the frequency domain, and find that it improves the video quality by alleviating the spectral energy drift to some extent, but cannot fully resolve it. Motivated by the above analysis, we propose FreqForcing, a training-free framework that addresses error accumulation in long-video generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring (SSA). The proposed SSA leverages the low-frequency components of anchor attention to maintain long-horizon visual stability, while preserving dynamic motion through the high-frequency components of local attention. Our FreqForcing extends Self-Forcing pretrained on 5s clips to two-minute generation, achieving 24x extrapolation. Extensive experiments show that FreqForcing outperforms existing training-free methods quantitatively and qualitatively while remaining competitive with representative training-based approaches.

</details>


### [78] [Veritas++: Value-aware On-Policy Distillation for Perception-Enhanced AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27113)
*Hao Tan,Jun Lan,Zichang Tan,Ajian Liu,Zijian Yu,Chuanbiao Song,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Wan,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Veritas++提出了一种增强感知的推理框架，通过强化对细粒度视觉细节、语义异常和像素级差异的感知能力，提升AI生成图像（AIGI）检测的鲁棒性和泛化性。其核心包括感知导向学习（PoRL）与价值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD），前者以可验证奖励替代开放描述监督，后者通过自教师机制优先整合高价值信号，实现感知与推理的深度融合。实验表明，该方法在标准、真实场景及新兴基准上均表现优异，显著缩小感知差距并持续优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的AIGI检测存在感知瓶颈，主要关注视觉证据的组织与合成，而忽视内在感知能力的优化，导致对细微异常的捕捉不足，亟需一种更可靠、更具感知能力的检测框架。

Method: 提出Veritas++框架，包含两项关键技术：1）感知导向学习（PoRL），用可验证奖励替代开放式描述监督，显式强化细粒度视觉细节、语义异常和像素级差异的感知能力；2）价值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD），通过自教师机制动态选择高价值蒸馏信号，实现感知增强的推理能力内化。

Result: 在多个标准、真实场景及新兴基准测试中，Veritas++展现出卓越的泛化能力，感知学习有效弥合了感知差距，带来检测性能的无缝提升；VaOPD实现了高效的能力演进，同时保持原有性能不下降。

Conclusion: Veritas++通过将可靠感知作为真实性推理的基础，成功构建了一个感知与推理深度融合的AIGI检测框架，为未来通用、鲁棒的AI生成内容检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The growing capability of image generation models has made synthetic images a routine presence in open media, making robust and generalizable AI-Generated Image (AIGI) detection increasingly essential. While multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) offer a transparent alternative to black-box binary scoring, we observe that current MLLM-based detectors still exhibit notable perception bottlenecks in capturing fine-grained anomalies. They primarily focus on how visual evidence is organized and synthesized, leaving the intrinsic perception less optimized. To mitigate this gap, we present Veritas++, a perception-enhanced reasoning framework that establishes reliable perception as the foundation of authenticity reasoning. Rather than directly optimizing the model's explanatory ability, we ground AIGI detection on three basic perception abilities, i.e., capturing fine-grained visual details, semantic anomalies and pixel-level differences. Building on this insight, we introduce Perception-oriented Learning (PoRL), which replaces open-ended description supervision with verifiable rewards to explicitly strengthen these capacities. To further integrate enhanced perception with reasoning, we introduce Value-aware On-Policy Distillation (VaOPD), an adaptive distillation mechanism that prioritizes high-value distillation signals over uniform supervision, internalizing perception-aware reasoning through a privileged self-teacher. Extensive experiments across standard, in-the-wild and emerging benchmarks demonstrate that Veritas++ achieves promising generalization. The perception learning effectively bridges the perception gap and yields seamless gains on detection, while VaOPD further enables efficient capability evolvement without sacrificing existing performance. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/EricTan7/VeritasPP.

</details>


### [79] [Towards Grounded GI Endoscopy VQA via Multi-Task Learning on Small VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27122)
*Itbaan Safwan,Ramail Khan,Muhammad Annas Shaikh,Muhammad Atif Tahir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的多任务微调方法，通过复用专家标注的息肉掩码和基于Grad-CAM的弱监督信号，在无需大量额外标注的情况下，提升视觉问答模型在胃肠内镜图像分析中的答案准确率与可解释性。实验表明，该方法在多个小型VLM模型上均取得一致性能提升，并增强了答案与图像相关区域之间的隐式对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前胃肠道内镜图像分析从单标签分类转向视觉问答（VQA），但临床应用不仅需要高准确率，还需模型内部表示能反映其决策所依据的视觉证据，即提高模型的可解释性。现有方法在准确性上表现良好，但在视觉证据对齐方面仍有不足。

Method: 提出一种多任务微调策略，利用现有VQA数据集中的专家标注掩码作为辅助接地任务，对缺乏真实掩码的类别则使用基于梯度加权类激活映射（Grad-CAM）的弱监督信号生成伪掩码，构建描述与定位任务。在小规模VLM模型上采用低秩适应（LoRA）进行微调，对比仅优化VQA任务的基线方法。

Result: 在Kvasir-VQA-x1数据集上的实验显示，多任务微调方法在所有模型上均实现更高的答案准确率，且在分布内和分布外数据上都表现出更强的答案与图像区域间的隐式对齐能力，证明了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入轻量级的多任务学习框架并有效利用现有标注资源与弱监督信号，本方法显著提升了视觉问答模型在胃肠道内镜图像分析中的性能与可解释性，为临床部署提供了更可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopic image analysis has shifted from single-label classification toward visual question answering (VQA), where a model must answer free-form clinical questions about an image. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) achieve promising answer accuracy on this task, clinical adoption also requires the model's internal representations to reflect the visual evidence behind its answers. We propose a simple multi-task fine-tuning recipe that constructs auxiliary grounding and description tasks from an existing VQA dataset with minimal additional annotation: expert-annotated polyp masks are reused directly, while a GI-domain pretrained classifier with Grad-CAM localization provides weak supervision for finding categories that lack ground-truth masks. Three small VLM backbones are fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation under matched VQA-only and multi-task recipes on Kvasir-VQA-x1, and we show consistent accuracy gains together with improved implicit alignment between answer tokens and the relevant image region, evaluated on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [80] [SeasonStereo: Robust Dense Stereo Matching for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery via Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27139)
*Álvaro Díaz-Laureano,Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Pablo Arias,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeasonStereo提出了一种可扩展的框架，通过在具有受控季节性外观变化的合成图像对上训练，并利用基础模型的零样本几何先验，解决异时卫星图像的视差估计问题。该方法在无需对齐的真实多日期训练数据或LiDAR标签的情况下，达到了与最先进的LiDAR监督模型相当的精度，并生成更清晰的几何细节，显著降低了大规模3D重建的监督成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D重建依赖于近同时的立体像对，难以应用于多日期影像存在季节和光照差异的场景。获取对齐的多日期真实影像及真实几何标注成本高昂，因此亟需一种能应对外观变化、减少监督依赖的解决方案。

Method: 采用合成图像对进行训练，控制季节性外观变化；结合零样本几何先验（来自基础模型），提升模型对真实异时影像的适应能力。

Result: 在不使用真实对齐多日期训练数据或LiDAR标签的前提下，达到与先进LiDAR监督模型相当的3D重建精度，且几何细节更清晰。

Conclusion: SeasonStereo为异时卫星影像的大规模高精度3D重建提供了低成本、可扩展的实用路径。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction from satellite imagery typically relies on near-simultaneous stereo pairs, limiting its applicability to diachronic settings where multi-date images exhibit varying seasonal and illumination conditions. Training dense stereo matching models robust to appearance changes is a long-standing challenge, as aligned multi-date imagery and ground-truth geometry are costly to obtain at scale. We propose SeasonStereo, a scalable framework that addresses disparity estimation from diachronic satellite images by training on synthetic image pairs with controlled seasonal appearance variation, while leveraging zero-shot geometric priors from foundation models. SeasonStereo matches the accuracy of state-of-the-art LiDAR-supervised models, while producing sharper geometric details without requiring aligned real multi-date training products or LiDAR-derived labels. As a result, SeasonStereo offers a practical path toward large-scale 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous satellite images with reduced supervision cost.

</details>


### [81] [Explainable and Resource-Efficient Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs for Decision-Critical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27145)
*Piyush Jain,Kousik Dasgupta,Rajarshi Roy,Subarna Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ByDeWay-V2 提出一种无需训练的框架，通过引入显式空间关系上下文（如“左/右”、“在...内”等可读谓词）增强多模态大模型的空间理解能力，解决现有方法在相同几何平面上物体间空间关系建模不足的问题。该框架结合YOLO-World-L检测对象并计算其成对几何关系，将结构化空间谓词注入提示中，提升模型推理准确性与可审计性。在VSR和BLINK基准测试中表现优异，相对LDP提升46%的F1分数，并在资源受限环境下保持高效运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在空间判断上存在透明度低、细粒度空间理解差及对象幻觉等问题，尤其在处理同一平面内的投影或拓扑空间关系时表现不佳。尽管已有方法如ByDeWay（LDP）利用深度图缓解幻觉，但其粗粒度分层仍无法精确刻画物体间的复杂空间关系，限制了在机器人、安全监控等关键决策场景中的可信度与可审计性。

Method: 提出ByDeWay-V2框架，使用开放词汇目标检测器YOLO-World-L提取图像中对象，计算对象之间的成对几何关系（如位置、包含、接触等），生成人类可读的空间谓词，并将其以结构化形式注入MLLM提示中，实现3D深度信息与2D空间语义的融合，全程无需微调或训练。

Result: 在BLINK空间子集上，ByDeWay-V2相较LDP使Qwen2.5-VL的F1得分提升46%；在VSR任务中，使BLIP-Base的性能从接近随机水平恢复至F1=0.53，达到竞争性水平。最轻配置仅需40个令牌上下文预算，在CPU上运行，适用于资源受限的实时决策支持系统。

Conclusion: ByDeWay-V2通过引入可解释的空间关系谓词，显著提升了MLLM在复杂空间推理任务中的准确性与可审计性，且无需训练、计算开销小，具备在实际部署中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in decision-critical pipelines such as robotics, embodied AI, and safety monitoring, the opacity of their spatial judgments limits operator trust and auditability. MLLMs demonstrate strong reasoning but often struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and object hallucination. Prior work, ByDeWay, introduced Layered-Depth-Based Prompting (LDP), a training-free framework that mitigates hallucinations by structuring prompts using monocular depth estimation. However, coarse depth layering falls short in resolving object-to-object spatial relationships within the same geometric plane, such as projective ("left of", "above") and topological ("inside", "touching") relations. We propose ByDeWay-V2, which integrates explicit spatial relational context alongside depth cues, expressed as human-readable predicates that serve as auditable evidence for downstream decision support. Using an open-vocabulary object detector (YOLO-World-L), our framework computes pairwise geometric relations between detected objects and injects them as structured spatial predicates into the MLLM prompt, bridging 3D scene depth and 2D spatial semantics without any training. We evaluate ByDeWay-V2 on the Visual Spatial Reasoning (VSR) and BLINK benchmarks across multiple MLLMs, with hallucination grounding assessed via POPE. On the BLINK spatial subset, ByDeWay-V2 achieves a 46 percent relative F1 improvement over LDP for Qwen2.5-VL, and recovers BLIP-Base's spatial reasoning on VSR from near-random performance to a competitive F1 of 0.53. Our lightest configuration operates under a strict 40-token context budget on CPU, showing the framework's suitability for resource-constrained, real-time decision-support settings.

</details>


### [82] [Anatomy Contextualized Adaption of CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27154)
*Roshan Kenia,Stephanie L McNamara,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级框架Anatomy Contextualized Adaptation (ACA)，用于在保持全局解剖上下文的同时，对冻结的CT视觉-语言基础模型进行解剖级对齐。该方法利用TotalSegmentator将CT体积分解为解剖级嵌入，并通过变压器捕捉跨解剖关系，同时与解剖级和扫描级文本对齐。在Merlin和CT-RATE数据集上，ACA在零样本发现分类任务中优于基线和现有细粒度方法，且训练时间少于一小时（嵌入缓存后）。注意力权重显示了合理的跨解剖上下文路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT视觉-语言基础模型使用整体体积表示，削弱了细粒度解剖信号；而现有的细粒度方法虽能增强解剖级对齐，但丢失了全局上下文，且通常从头训练，计算成本高。需要一种既能保留全局上下文又能实现细粒度对齐的高效方法。

Method: 采用TotalSegmentator将CT体积分解为解剖级嵌入，通过一个可学习的变压器对这些嵌入进行精炼以捕获跨解剖关系，并将其与来自放射科报告的解剖级和扫描级文本进行对齐。整个过程基于已冻结的基础模型，仅训练少量参数，实现轻量化适配。

Result: 在Merlin和CT-RATE数据集上，ACA在零样本发现分类任务中表现优于冻结的基础模型和现有细粒度方法；训练时间低于一小时（嵌入缓存后）；注意力权重揭示了合理的跨解剖上下文路由机制。

Conclusion: ACA是一种高效的轻量级方法，能够有效适应CT基础模型实现解剖级视觉-语言对齐，同时保留并增强全局解剖上下文，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: CT vision-language foundation models have demonstrated promising performance across downstream tasks, but are typically trained with whole-volume representations that dilute fine-grained anatomical signals. Fine-grained vision-language pre-training addresses this by aligning anatomy-level visual features with anatomy-specific text, but in doing so discards the global context that whole-volume models provide. Furthermore, existing fine-grained approaches train from scratch, making them computationally expensive. We introduce Anatomy Contextualized Adaptation (ACA), a lightweight framework that adapts frozen CT foundation model representations for anatomy-level vision-language alignment while enhancing global contextualization. ACA uses TotalSegmentator to decompose CT volumes into anatomy-level embeddings, which are refined via a transformer that captures cross-anatomy relationships, and aligned to both per-anatomy and scan-level text extracted from radiology reports. Evaluated on Merlin and CT-RATE, ACA consistently outperforms both the frozen foundation model baselines and existing fine-grained methods in zero-shot finding classification, while requiring less than one hour of training once embeddings are cached. The attention weights learned by ACA's inter-anatomy transformer additionally indicate plausible cross-anatomy context routing. Altogether, these results support ACA as a lightweight approach for adapting CT foundation models to anatomically grounded vision-language alignment while preserving and enhancing global anatomical context.

</details>


### [83] [HumanCLAW: Can Vision-Language Models Act Through a Body?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27180)
*Siyao Li,Jiawei Gu,Shuai Liu,Kairui Hu,Zekun Li,Linjie Li,Chengcheng Tang,Po-Chen Wu,Ivan Shugurov,Lingni Ma,Michael Zollhoefer,Sizhe An,Abhay Mittal,Amy Zhao,Ranjay Krishna,Manling Li,Ziwei Liu,Chuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HumanCLAW introduces a framework to evaluate vision-language models (VLMs) in physical tasks by decoupling action decision-making from low-level motor execution. It uses an off-the-shelf VLM to issue atomic skill commands, which are executed as continuous full-body motions with real-world physics. This isolates the model's decision-making ability from motor errors. The authors create HumanCLAW-Bench with 1,218 long-horizon, egocentric tasks across 41 indoor scenes. Testing nine top VLMs reveals poor performance (best: 16.8% success), indicating that current models lack embodied self-awareness—failing to track their body’s state, position, or interactions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current VLMs struggle to act through physical bodies because task failures stem from either poor decisions or motor control issues, making it hard to assess true action intelligence. A method is needed to isolate and measure decision-making ability independently of execution errors.

Method: HumanCLAW decouples action decisions from motor execution by using a VLM to issue atomic skill commands, which are translated into sub-second, physically realistic full-body motions. This allows the body to interact with the environment under real physics while minimizing execution-side noise like balance loss or motor errors.

Result: The best-performing VLM achieved only a 16.8% success rate on HumanCLAW-Bench, indicating significant limitations in current models. The main bottleneck is not perception but embodied self-awareness—models fail to track their own body’s state, location, goal attainment, or obstacles.

Conclusion: Current VLMs lack embodied self-awareness, limiting their ability to perform long-horizon physical tasks despite strong perception capabilities. HumanCLAW provides a robust evaluation framework to measure true action intelligence and guide future model development.

Abstract: Evaluating whether a vision-language model (VLM) can act through a physical body is challenging. The outcome of an action couples the VLM's decision with motor control. When a task fails, it is hard to tell whether the VLM made a bad choice or the motor controller simply failed to execute it, e.g., losing balance and falling. In this work, we introduce HumanCLAW, an evaluation framework that decouples action decision-making from low-level execution. At every step, a harnessed, off-the-shelf VLM issues an atomic skill command, and the command is translated into a sub-second chunk of continuous full-body motion with real physical consequences, including gravity and collisions. The body can therefore act freely in the physical world, while execution-side disturbances, balance and motor errors, are factored out. What remains measurable is the model's action intelligence: its moment-to-moment choice of what the body should execute next. Based on this framework, we build HumanCLAW-Bench: 1,218 long-horizon, egocentric find-navigate-interact episodes across 41 indoor scenes. We test nine state-of-the-art VLMs and find that none solves the benchmark; the best model reaches only a 16.8% success rate. Recognizing the target is not the bottleneck. What current VLMs lack is embodied self-awareness: they lose track of their own body, failing to tell where it is, whether it has reached the goal, or whether it has hit an obstacle.

</details>


### [84] [VidMap: Exploiting Temporal Structure for Video-Based Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27194)
*Zador Pataki,Paul-Edouard Sarlin,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合SLAM的序列约束与SfM的全局优化优势的新系统，用于实现任意长、未标定视频的度量重建。通过利用宽基线密集图像匹配技术，将时间顺序作为可靠回环闭合的关键，并引入度量单目深度先验增强全局优化，显著提升了在极端运动和视觉对称性场景下的鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM方法受限于初始化敏感性和实时性要求，而传统SfM虽能进行全局优化但对视觉对称性和极端运动缺乏鲁棒性。因此需要一种兼具两者优势的方法来实现大规模、未标定视频的准确相机标定与度量位姿恢复。

Method: 融合SLAM的时序约束与离线SfM的全局优化；采用宽基线密集图像匹配建立特征对应；以时间顺序为关键支持回环闭合；引入度量单目深度先验辅助全局优化。

Result: 在包含极端运动和视觉对称性的多样化挑战性数据集上，所提方法在鲁棒性和准确性方面均显著优于当前最先进的SLAM与SfM方法（无论是经典或学习型，已知或未知相机标定）。

Conclusion: 该系统成功实现了对任意长、未标定视频的高精度度量重建，为导航与场景理解提供了大规模训练数据的可能性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Accurately recovering the camera's calibration and metric poses for any unconstrained video would unlock large-scale training data for navigation and scene understanding. The dominant approaches to this problem are severely limited: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) is sensitive to initialization and transient failures due to its causal, incremental nature; it is often over-optimized for real-time operation and generally requires known camera calibration; while Structure-from-Motion (SfM) typically forgoes any image ordering, enabling optimal initialization and global optimization, but lacks robustness to visual symmetries and extreme motions. To bridge this gap, we introduce a system that combines the strong sequential constraints of SLAM with the flexibility and global optimization of offline SfM, enabling the metric reconstruction of arbitrary, long, uncalibrated videos. This system leverages recent advances in wide-baseline dense image matching, treats temporal ordering as a first-class citizen for reliable loop closure, and augments global optimization with metric monocular depth priors. As a result, thorough evaluations on diverse, challenging datasets that exhibit extreme motion and visual symmetries reveal that our approach is significantly more robust and accurate than both state-of-the-art SLAM and SfM, classical or learned, with given or unknown camera calibration. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/cvg/vidmap.

</details>


### [85] [TurboVLA: Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Model at 32 Hz on an RTX 4090 with <1 GB VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27205)
*Hengyi Xie,Chenfei Yao,Xianjin Wu,Xuanyang Xi,Yiping Tang,Di Xu,Yingying Zhu,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboVLA introduces a direct $V + L 	o A$ mapping for vision-language-action models, bypassing the LLM-centric $V 	o L 	o A$ pathway. It uses lightweight bidirectional vision-language interaction and a compact decoder to reduce computation and memory costs, achieving high performance with minimal resources.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The conventional LLM-centric VLA design incurs high computational and memory overhead during policy inference, limiting efficiency and deployment on consumer hardware. This work aims to develop a more efficient alternative without sacrificing performance.

Method: TurboVLA independently encodes visual and language inputs, enables lightweight bidirectional interaction between them, and directly predicts continuous actions via a compact decoder, avoiding reliance on large language models as intermediaries.

Result: On LIBERO, TurboVLA achieves 97.7% average success rate using only 0.2B parameters, 31.2 ms inference latency, and 0.9 GB VRAM on an RTX 4090, outperforming or matching larger VLA models in efficiency and effectiveness.

Conclusion: TurboVLA presents a simple yet effective paradigm shift in VLA design, enabling efficient robotic manipulation by directly linking vision and language to action, offering a practical alternative to LLM-heavy architectures.

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models commonly adopt an LLM-centric $V \to L \to A$ pathway, where visual observations are projected into the representation space of a large language model before being decoded into robot actions. Although effective, this design incurs substantial computation and memory overhead at every policy invocation. In this work, we introduce TurboVLA, a new VLA paradigm that reformulates the conventional $V \to L \to A$ pathway as a direct $V + L \to A$ mapping. Instead of using a large language model as the central interface between perception and action, TurboVLA independently encodes visual observations and language instructions, directly exchanges information between them through lightweight bidirectional vision-language interaction, and predicts continuous action chunks with a compact decoder. This simple design constructs task-conditioned representations directly from visual and linguistic features, significantly reducing the computational and memory costs of VLA inference. On LIBERO, TurboVLA achieves 97.7% average success with only 0.2B parameters, 31.2 ms inference latency, and 0.9 GB inference VRAM on a consumer-grade RTX 4090, matching or outperforming substantially larger VLA policies. These results establish TurboVLA as a simple and effective alternative to the prevailing LLM-centric VLA paradigm, offering a new perspective on how vision, language, and action can be connected for efficient robotic manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/TurboVLA.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Large-Scale ChatBot Validation Through Customer Digital Twin Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26060)
*Cristovao Iglesias,Devesh Batra,Alankar Atreya,Stefan Wagner,Robert Hankache,Patrick Sinclair,Giulio Pelosio,Michael McMillan,Greig A. Cowan,Raad Khraishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的聊天机器人大规模验证方法，包含两个核心部分：一是构建高保真度的合成客户代理（SCAs），利用真实交易和对话数据生成数字孪生客户，可模拟多样化客户画像与交互风格；二是建立基于SCA的综合验证框架，结合LLM作为裁判、人工专家测试和对抗性探查，实现跨情绪状态、人口统计特征和语言因素的场景化评估。该方法在英国某领先银行的客户面向聊天机器人中成功应用，为金融机构提供了一条可扩展、合规的部署路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的聊天机器人虽在金融等受监管领域改变客户服务模式，但缺乏高效且低成本的大规模验证手段，制约其安全部署。亟需一种能模拟真实客户行为、支持可控干预并满足监管要求的自动化验证机制。

Method: 提出合成客户代理（SCA）生成方法，基于真实数据构建数字孪生客户，支持行为条件化与个性特征控制；设计融合LLM-as-a-Judge、人类专家测试与对抗性探查的验证框架，实现多维度场景下的系统性评估。

Result: SCAs在语义一致性、幻觉率和人格特征再现方面表现优异，具备可控干预能力；验证框架在多种情境下均展现出稳健性能，已成功应用于英国某大型银行的聊天机器人验证，推动其向监管合规迈进。

Conclusion: 所提出的SCA驱动验证方法为金融等行业中的聊天机器人提供了可扩展、低成本且符合监管要求的验证路径，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: LLM-based chatbots are transforming customer service in regulated domains such as banking, but scalable and cost-effective validation remains a critical barrier to safe deployment. We present a two-part contribution for large-scale chatbot validation. First, we introduce a methodology for creating high-fidelity synthetic customer agents (SCAs) as digital twins, grounded in real transactional and conversational data, that enables automatic generation and behavioral conditioning to simulate diverse customer profiles and interaction styles. Evaluation demonstrates that SCAs achieve high semantic alignment with real customers, low hallucination rates, and successful personality trait reproduction with controllable interventions. Second, we develop an SCA-based validation framework combining automated LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation, human expert testing, and adversarial probing. Scenario-based validation across emotional states, demographic groups, and linguistic factors confirms robust performance. Our approach was used to validate a customer facing chatbot at a leading UK bank, providing financial institutions with a scalable pathway toward regulatory compliance.

</details>


### [87] [Do Methods Support the Claims? Intra-Paper Verification for Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26066)
*Ranjitha Shivaprasad Ballakuraya,Arash Mahyari,Ashok Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“论文内主张验证”的框架，旨在评估学术论文中宣称的创新性贡献是否被其方法论实际支持。现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动评审系统通常仅比较贡献与已有文献，忽略了方法与主张之间的内在不一致问题。该框架通过LLM提取引言中的创新主张，检索相关方法证据，并依据从182篇ICLR 2025论文的人类评审中归纳出的评价标准，判断方法是否支撑主张。实验表明，该框架生成的评论与人类评审意见高度一致，尤其在创新性问题上表现良好，且通过BERTScore验证了人机评论对的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的自动评审系统往往假设论文中宣称的贡献是真实实现的，但人类审稿人常因方法论证据不足而质疑创新性主张。这种‘主张与方法不匹配’的问题未被现有系统充分关注，因此需要一种能识别内部一致性问题的新方法。

Method: 提出‘论文内主张验证’框架，利用LLM从论文引言中提取创新主张，检索与主张相关的具体方法内容，结合从182篇ICLR 2025论文人类评审中归纳出的评价标准（涵盖新颖性、方法、清晰度等），生成结构化的审查式评估报告。

Result: 人类评估显示，该框架生成的评论与人类审稿人关切高度一致，尤其是在新颖性方面；BERTScore分析进一步证实了人机评论对之间存在显著一致性，优于随机对照组，说明该框架有效捕捉了真实审稿关注点。

Conclusion: 本文提出的内文主张验证框架能够有效识别论文中‘声称贡献’与‘方法实现’之间的不一致，弥补了现有LLM评审系统在评估内部逻辑一致性方面的不足，为构建更贴近人类审稿行为的自动化评审工具提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The growing volume of scientific submissions has motivated interest in using large language models (LLMs) to assist peer review. Existing automated novelty assessment approaches typically compare a paper's claimed contributions against prior literature, implicitly assuming that these contributions are accurately realized in the work itself. Human reviewers, however, frequently challenge novelty claims not because similar ideas already exist, but because the methodological evidence presented in the paper does not adequately support them. This internal mismatch between claimed contributions and methodological realization is rarely examined by current LLM-based review systems. To address this gap, we introduce intra-paper claim verification, a framework that evaluates whether novelty claims articulated in a paper are substantiated by the methods used to realize them. The framework employs an LLM to extract novelty claims from the introduction, retrieve claim-relevant methodological evidence, and assess whether the methods substantiate the stated contributions. Assessment is guided by reviewer-inspired evaluation criteria derived inductively from human peer reviews collected from 182 ICLR 2025 papers. These criteria capture recurring reviewer concerns related to novelty, methodology, clarity, and other issues and are used to generate structured reviewer-style assessments of claim substantiation. We evaluate the framework by comparing LLM-generated review comments against human reviewer concerns on a balanced subset of accepted and rejected papers. Human evaluation demonstrates significant alignment between framework-generated assessments and human reviewer concerns, particularly for novelty-related issues. BERTScore further distinguishes corresponding human-LLM review pairs from mismatched controls, indicating that the framework captures concerns consistent with human reviewer observations.

</details>


### [88] [DuplexGen: Adaptive Synthesis of Human-AI Turn-Taking Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26178)
*Takyoung Kim,Kang-wook Kim,Sang Hoon Woo,Julia Hirschberg,Gunhee Kim,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexGen框架，通过少量人类偏好标注校准大模型的对话生成，实现场景自适应的对话轮换行为。在六种合作与竞争任务中，该方法显著优于未校准提示或仅依赖通用语料训练的模型，且基于其生成数据训练的全双工模型展现出更符合人类偏好的轮换行为，证明人类校准是实现场景化轮换的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话模型在不同场景下采用统一的轮换规则，但实际中合适的轮换行为应随场景变化；现有方法受限于训练数据缺乏角色定位和场景规范，或依赖启发式生成，无法反映人类真实偏好。

Method: 提出DuplexGen框架，利用少量槽位级人类偏好标注对大语言模型的轮换预测进行校准，使生成对话的轮换行为适配具体任务场景。

Result: 在六类任务中，人类偏好差异明显；经校准的DuplexGen生成的对话更贴近人类偏好，优于无校准提示或仅用通用语料训练的模型；基于其生成数据训练的全双工模型表现出独特且受人类青睐的轮换行为。

Conclusion: 人类校准而非语料规模或提示设计本身，是实现对话轮换行为场景特异性的关键因素。

Abstract: Turn-taking is a central component of full-duplex interaction. Which turn-taking behaviors are appropriate varies with the scenario, yet current models apply a single norm regardless of context. This limitation originates in their training data: human-human speech corpora capture natural timing phenomena but provide little role grounding or scenario-specific norms, while heuristic or prompted synthesis methods inject turn-taking behaviors without basing them on human preferences. We introduce DuplexGen, a framework for generating dialogues with scenario-adaptive turn-taking by calibrating LLM predictions against a small set of slot-level human preference annotations. In six cooperative and competitive tasks, human turn-taking preferences differ systematically, and DuplexGen aligns substantially more closely with those preferences than uncalibrated prompting or training solely on generic human-human data; a full-duplex model trained on DuplexGen-generated data exhibits distinctive, human-preferred turn-taking behaviors. These results show that human calibration, not corpus scale or prompt design alone, is what allows turn-taking synthesis to be scenario-specific.

</details>


### [89] [Choosing Where and How to Moderate: End-to-End Trade-offs in Filter Placement and Response Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26200)
*Mengya Hu,Susie Park,Suzana Ilic,Qiong Wei,Sandeep Atluri,Myra Deng,Tucker Fross,Curt Tigges*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估内容审核分类器在实际部署中的选择，提出使用端到端客户结果指标（如有用性与有害暴露）而非仅依赖组件准确率。比较了输入仅、响应仅及输入+响应硬拦截策略，发现响应仅在有用性上表现最佳，而输入+响应组合能降低有害暴露。通过响应重写可恢复大部分被拦截流量，且保持相同有害暴露水平，但该结果非等价性结论。探针路由显著降低延迟，同时维持良好性能。输出审查显示重写在保留良性意图的同时泛化触发语言，但某些敏感领域仍可能丢失安全相关信息。研究强调应根据具体部署的安全与延迟约束评估配置，而非采用统一规则。代码与数据公开于GitHub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核系统通常孤立评估分类器性能，但实际部署中需考虑干预位置及后续操作的影响。为更真实反映用户体验与安全性，需引入端到端的客户结果指标进行评估。

Method: 采用两个端到端指标：有用性（展示的非有害相关回复比例）和有害暴露（展示的有害回复比例），对比输入仅、响应仅、输入+响应硬拦截三种策略，并结合响应重写机制测试效果；使用人类标注的产品基准和公共 ToxicChat 数据集进行评估。还引入探针路由以优化生成延迟。

Result: 响应仅策略在有用性上最优；输入+响应组合在有害暴露上最低；响应+重写可恢复大部分被拦截流量，且有害暴露量与响应仅策略相当，但不具等价性；探针路由显著降低条件路由与生成时间，同时保持相近性能。输出审查表明重写有效平衡通过率与有用性，但在敏感领域可能遗漏关键安全信息。

Conclusion: 内容审核配置的选择应基于具体部署场景下的安全性和延迟约束，而非通用规则。建议结合实际业务需求评估不同策略，以实现更好的用户体验与安全性平衡。

Abstract: Content-moderation classifiers are usually evaluated in isolation, but deployment requires choosing where to intervene and what follows a flag. We evaluate these choices using two end-to-end customer-outcome metrics rather than component accuracy: Usefulness, the fraction of turns with a shown, non-harmful, relevant response, and Harmful Exposure, the fraction with a shown harmful response. Latency and error rates are diagnostics. We compare Input only, Response only, and Input + response hard blocking on a human-labelled product benchmark and public ToxicChat evaluation. At the evaluated operating points, Response only achieves the highest filter-only Usefulness in both settings, while Input + response achieves lower Harmful Exposure. Replacing Response only blocking with Response + rewrite recovers most blocked traffic and yields the same observed Harmful Exposure count as Response only blocking for the selected configuration; this equality is not an equivalence result. Probe routing substantially reduces conditional route-and-generation time relative to LLM routing at comparable measured outcomes. A focused output review shows how rewrites balance filter passage with usefulness by generalizing triggering language while retaining benign intent and safe redirection; some sensitive-domain outputs nevertheless omit potentially safety-relevant support information. These results support comparing moderation configurations under deployment-specific safety and latency constraints rather than applying a universal placement rule. Code and public artifacts are available at https://github.com/microsoft/mod-frontier

</details>


### [90] [Characterizing Human-Likeness in AI Generated Poetry: A Zero-shot Classification Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26221)
*A. N. Biswas,T. Tabassum,A. A. Shohid,R. M. Mou,A. A. Esha,F. Sadeque,A. Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究旨在探究英文诗歌中影响人类与生成式人工智能（GenAI）诗歌分类及误分类的关键属性，提出一种零样本检测流程，利用包含人类与AI生成诗歌的数据集验证其可区分性，并提取有助于准确分类的重要特征。该方法可减少训练需求，仅需对误分类特征的诗歌进行微调，同时为增强现代检测系统提供关键洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI技术的发展，人类与AI生成文本已几乎无法区分，尤其在诗歌领域，现有研究表明未经修改的AI诗歌最难被检测，且现代检测工具对诗歌的主观性与大语言模型的黑箱特性存在局限，因此亟需验证此类作品的客观可区分性。

Method: 提出一种零样本检测管道，结合人类与AI生成的诗歌数据集，通过分析诗歌特征实现分类与误分类属性的提取。

Result: 成功识别出影响诗歌分类与误分类的关键属性，证明了部分诗歌在当前检测系统下仍具可区分性；同时表明仅针对误分类特征进行微调即可显著提升检测效率与准确性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了生成式AI诗歌与人类创作在特定语义和结构特征上的差异，为优化当前检测机制提供了实证支持，有助于构建更精准、高效的生成内容检测体系。

Abstract: With the advancement of AI technologies, Generative AI (GenAI) and human written text have become nearly indistinguishable. Additionally, the global standardization of AI chatbots made academic malpractice more frequent. Furthermore, existing research indicates GenAI poems are the most difficult to distinguish even without any modification thus, GenAI poems are naturally deemed human-like by modern detectors. However, the objectivity of such dissertations needs to be verified against modern detection tools but the subjectivity of poetry and the black-box nature of the modern LLMs (Large Language Models) architectures made verification of such work quite complicated. Hence, the main objective of the research is to deduce the attributes of English poetry that contribute classification and misclassification of both human and AI poems and provide corroborating or contradicting evidence to the poetry distinguishability claim. For such characterizations, we propose a Zero-shot detection pipeline with a dataset consisting of both human and AI poems to verify the distinguishability of human and AI creation and extract the aforementioned crucial attributes for accurate classification. Extraction of such attributes provides benefits in two ways: firstly, it reduces the margin of training needed as only the poems based on misclassifying attributes need to be trained and fine tuned and finally provides a critical insight to the GenAI detection dilemma to strengthen the modern detection pipelines.

</details>


### [91] [Steering Instruction Hierarchies at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26228)
*Siqi Zeng,Sewoong Lee,Han Zhao,Julia Hockenmaier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: V-Steer是一种无需训练的推理时方法，通过在提示位置编辑缓存的值向量来恢复优先级影响，利用直接逻辑归因识别主导低优先级片段的注意力头，并通过乘法编辑增强高优先级片段、抑制冲突的低优先级片段。该方法仅作用于缓存值，兼容融合注意力后端，仅增加一次预填充开销。在7B至70B模型上，其将主要约束准确率从不足18%提升至92%，在指令层次评估中显著优于仅提示基线，且在3/4规模模型上达到或超过现有基于训练的方法，解码速度开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿大语言模型常违反指令层级安全假设，即高优先级输入（如系统提示）应覆盖低优先级输入（如用户或工具输入），因此需要一种有效方法在不重新训练的情况下恢复优先级控制。

Method: 通过直接对首个下一个词的逻辑归因识别关键注意力头，在提示位置对缓存的值向量进行乘法编辑，增强高优先级片段并抑制低优先级冲突内容，整个过程在推理时完成且无需训练。

Result: 在7B到70B模型上，主约束准确率从低于18%提升至92%；在更广泛的指令层级测试中显著优于仅提示基线，且在3/4模型规模上达到或超过最先进训练方法，解码速度几乎无额外开销。

Conclusion: V-Steer提供了一种高效、兼容性强、无需训练的推理时方法，成功恢复了语言模型中的指令层级安全机制，适用于大规模模型部署。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchies are a core safety assumption of language model deployment: higher priority inputs, such as system prompts, should override conflicting lower priority inputs from users or tools. Yet frontier LLMs often violate this hierarchy. We introduce V-Steer, a training-free inference time method that restores privileged influence by editing cached value vectors at prompt positions. Using direct logit attribution on the first next token prediction, V-Steer identifies heads where lower priority spans dominate privileged ones, then boosts privileged spans and suppresses conflicting lower priority spans through in-place multiplicative edits to cached V tensors. Since the method acts only on cached values, it remains compatible with fused attention backends and adds only a one time prefill overhead. Across models from 7B to 70B, this attribution guided intervention raises primary constraint accuracy from under 18% up to 92% on controlled role conflict benchmarks, and on broader instruction hierarchy evaluations substantially outperforms prompt only baselines while matching or exceeding SoTA training based methods on 3 of 4 scales of LLMs, with negligible decoding-speed overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/cindy2000sh/v-steer.

</details>


### [92] [A large-scale corpus of religious radio broadcast transcripts from webstream recordings in the United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26249)
*Samuel Bestvater,Athena Chapekis,Skyler Seets,Anna Lieb,Sono Shah,Aaron Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该数据描述介绍了一个大规模的英文宗教广播转录语料库，涵盖2025年7月一个月内从785个直播流中捕获的15分钟片段，覆盖超过2000个AM/FM电台，生成超过70万条录音和超过6000万条标注语音行。所有录音通过自动化流程完成转录、说话人分离，并利用大语言模型进行节目格式与主题的分段标注。语料库以关联表格形式组织，包含流元数据、录制元数据和转录行信息，支持对宗教广播的区域与传统差异研究、社会政治议题在宗教媒体中的讨论分析以及该领域稀缺的语音处理研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宗教广播在美国广泛存在但研究不足，且内容分析受限于缺乏大规模转录数据。本研究旨在构建一个大规模、可访问的宗教广播语料库，以推动该领域的系统性研究。

Method: 通过滚动方式从785个在线直播流中捕获15分钟片段，覆盖超过2000个电台；使用自动化管道对录音进行转录与说话人分离；利用大语言模型对内容进行节目格式与主题的分段与标注；最终将数据组织为结构化链接表格。

Result: 成功构建了一个包含超过70万条录音和超过6000万条标注语音行的宗教广播语料库，具备丰富的元数据和细粒度标注，适用于多维度研究。

Conclusion: 该语料库填补了宗教广播研究中大规模转录数据的空白，为传播学、社会学及语音处理研究提供了重要资源，具有广泛的学术应用价值。

Abstract: Religious radio is a widespread but understudied form of mass communication in the United States, and content-level analysis of it has been constrained by the absence of large-scale transcript data. This Data Descriptor presents a corpus of transcribed English-language religious radio broadcasts captured from live webstreams over a one-month period in July 2025. Fifteen-minute segments were recorded on a rolling schedule from 785 distinct streams, which together rebroadcast the signals of more than two thousand AM and FM stations, yielding over 700,000 recordings and more than 60 million diarized lines of speech. Each recording was transcribed and speaker-diarized with an automated pipeline, and segmented and labeled by programming format and topic using a large language model. The corpus is organized as linked tables of stream metadata, recording metadata, and transcript lines. It supports descriptive study of religious broadcasting across regions and traditions, analysis of how social and political issues are discussed in religious media, and speech-processing research in an underrepresented domain.

</details>


### [93] [Robostreet Flow: A Lightweight, Ultra-Low-Drag Electric Tractor and Four-Truck Hybrid Convoy Architecture for Minimum-Cost Point-to-Point Freight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26250)
*Wei Wang,Yiru Veronika Wang,Sumukh Veeramalla,Xiaohui Liang,for the Robostreet Research Team*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Robostreet Flow提出了一种新型货运架构，通过联合优化车辆设计、车队编组和运营模式，在高密度点对点路线上显著降低每吨英里的成本。该系统采用电池电动6x4牵引车，配备流线型单座驾驶室（阻力系数0.35），碳纤维一体化车身与集成电池使整车重量减轻50%。搭配513 kWh牵引车电池和340 kWh拖车电池，实现500英里续航。四辆货车组成协同车队，仅领头车有安全驾驶员，其余三辆以SAE Level 4自动驾驶运行。计算流体动力学模拟显示，8米间距紧密跟驰可使跟随车辆阻力系数降低42-48%，前部峰值压力下降约四倍。能量模型预测车队平均能耗为1.27 kWh/mi，较单个车辆的1.60 kWh/mi降低20.5%。电力成本约为柴油的17%。通过四车共用一名司机并利用轻量化增加载重，运营成本从9.4美分/吨英里降至4.1美分，降幅达56%。研究还包含敏感性分析、枢纽到枢纽运营概念及监管影响评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公路货运成本主要由能源、司机人工和设备三部分构成，现有技术通常只针对单一因素进行优化，难以实现整体成本最小化。为突破这一局限，亟需一种能够协同优化车辆设计、车队运行方式与运营模式的新架构，以实现更高效、低成本的长途货运。

Method: 结合计算流体动力学（CFD）模拟、纵向能量建模、车辆结构轻量化设计以及自动驾驶车队协同控制策略，构建并评估了名为Robostreet Flow的新型货运系统。重点分析了紧凑队列行驶下的空气动力学优势，评估了电池配置、重量减轻、多车共用司机等带来的综合效益，并通过仿真验证其在实际运输场景中的性能表现。

Result: Robostreet Flow系统在高密度点对点运输中实现了显著的成本节约：能耗降低20.5%，电力成本仅为柴油的17%；通过共享司机和轻量化设计，运营成本从9.4美分/吨英里降至4.1美分，降幅达56%。同时，车队编组带来显著的空气动力学增益，提升整体能效。

Conclusion: Robostreet Flow展示了通过跨维度协同优化（车辆、车队、运营）实现货运系统根本性降本的可行性，具备在特定高流量走廊大规模部署的潜力，为未来零排放、高效率公路货运提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Line-haul trucking costs are dominated by three comparably sized components: energy, driver labor, and equipment. Most efficiency technologies address only one component at a time. This paper presents Robostreet Flow, a freight architecture that jointly optimizes the vehicle, convoy formation, and operating model to minimize cost per ton-mile on high-volume point-to-point corridors. The Flow platform is a battery-electric 6x4 tractor with a teardrop single-seat cab and a drag coefficient of 0.35, approximately 40% below that of conventional Class 8 tractors. A carbon-composite monocoque and structurally integrated batteries reduce net vehicle weight by 50%. A 513 kWh tractor battery and a 340 kWh powered trailer battery provide a 500-mile single-charge range. Four Flow trucks operate as a coordinated convoy with a safety driver only in the lead vehicle, while three followers operate in SAE Level 4 automated mode. Computational fluid dynamics simulations show that close following at an 8 m gap reduces follower drag coefficients by 42-48% and follower peak frontal pressure by approximately a factor of four relative to the exposed lead vehicle. A longitudinal energy model calibrated to these results predicts fleet-average consumption of 1.27 kWh/mi in convoy, compared with 1.60 kWh/mi for an isolated vehicle, for a 20.5% energy saving. Electricity cost is approximately 17% of the equivalent diesel fuel cost. Amortizing one driver across four trucks and accounting for the additional payload enabled by lightweighting reduce operating cost from 9.4 to 4.1 cents per ton-mile, a 56% reduction relative to a diesel baseline. Sensitivity analysis, a hub-to-hub operating concept, and regulatory implications are also presented.

</details>


### [94] [Evaluating Prompt Scope and Demonstration Similarity in Local LLM Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26286)
*Mihael Arcan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在机器翻译中的表现，重点关注提示范围和示例选择策略。在FLORES开发测试集上评估了九种欧盟官方语言的英-罗曼语族和英-日耳曼语族翻译任务，比较了三种本地指令微调的LLM（llama3.2:3b, mistral:latest, qwen2.5:14b）与专用MT基线（OPUS-MT、NLLB-200）。实验涵盖零样本和k=5少样本提示，使用随机、词汇相似性和嵌入相似性选取示例，并对比单目标提示与请求整个语系语言的JSON格式家族范围提示。结果显示：专用MT系统整体仍最强，尤其在日耳曼语族；少样本提示对mistral:latest和qwen2.5:14b有帮助，但损害llama3.2:3b的表现；嵌入检索平均最优，但优于其他方法的提升有限；家族范围提示对较强LLM可行，但在小型模型中暴露结构输出失败。研究强调应从提示范围、检索策略和多目标一致性等维度综合评估LLM翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在机器翻译中虽被广泛使用，但其评估通常局限于单一提示形式（如单句单语言翻译），而实际应用中用户可能要求多语言同时翻译或基于示例的条件翻译。因此，有必要系统研究提示范围和演示选择策略对本地LLM翻译效果的影响，以更全面地评估其实际可用性。

Method: 在FLORES devtest全集上进行英-罗曼语族和英-日耳曼语族翻译任务，使用三种本地指令微调的LLM（llama3.2:3b, mistral:latest, qwen2.5:14b）与两个专用MT基线（OPUS-MT, NLLB-200）对比。采用零样本和k=5少样本提示，示例选择方式包括随机、词汇相似性、嵌入相似性。同时比较单目标提示与JSON格式的家族范围提示（一次性请求一个语系内所有语言）。

Result: 专用MT系统整体表现最佳，尤其在德国语族上；少样本提示对mistral:latest和qwen2.5:14b有提升作用，但对llama3.2:3b产生负面影响；嵌入相似性示例选择在强模型上表现最优，但优势不显著；家族范围提示对强模型可行，但小模型易出现结构输出错误。

Conclusion: LLM在机器翻译中的表现不仅取决于语言对和评估指标，还受提示范围、示例检索策略和多目标一致性的显著影响。未来评估应综合考虑这些因素，以更真实反映其实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose translation systems, but their behavior is usually evaluated under a single prompt shape: translate one source sentence into one target language. In practice, users may ask for one target language, for several related languages at once, or for translations conditioned on examples. This paper studies prompt scope and demonstration selection as experimental variables for local LLM machine translation. We evaluate English-to-Romance and English-to-Germanic translation on the full FLORES devtest split for nine official European Union languages. We compare three local instruction-tuned LLMs, llama3.2:3b, mistral:latest, and qwen2.5:14b, against dedicated MT baselines from OPUS-MT and NLLB-200. We test zero-shot prompting and k=5 few-shot prompting with random, lexical-similarity, and embedding-similarity demonstration selection. We also compare single-target prompts with JSON-formatted family-scope prompts that request all languages in a family at once. Results show that dedicated MT systems remain strongest overall, especially for Germanic languages. Few-shot prompting helps mistral:latest and qwen2.5:14b, but hurts llama3.2:3b; embedding retrieval is best on average for the stronger LLMs, but its advantage over random and lexical examples is modest. Family-scope prompting is feasible for stronger local LLMs but exposes structured-output failures in smaller models. These findings motivate evaluating LLM translation not only by language pair and metric, but also by prompt scope, retrieval strategy, and multi-target compliance.

</details>


### [95] [AgentGUI: An Interface for Observing and Steering Long-Running AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26300)
*Xuan Zhao,Jiwoong Sohn,Qinyue Zheng,Michael Moor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentGUI 是一个本地托管的用户友好图形界面，用于无缝观察和控制多个并发、长时间运行的AI代理会话。它提供丰富的代理轨迹可视化、有效的手动与自动干预功能，并能集成和协调开源及前沿代理框架。用户研究显示，使用AgentGUI可使识别代理轨迹关键元素的时间减少38%（p=0.023）；初步实验表明，其自动化漂移预防功能可将小型本地代理的任务完成率提升最高达34个百分点（在0.8B至9B模型范围内，每模型50次运行）。项目已公开发布，包含网站、开源仓库及演示视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理自主能力的快速提升，人类监督因缺乏以用户为中心的交互界面而滞后，亟需一种高效、直观的工具来支持对复杂长时任务中多代理行为的观察与干预。

Method: 设计并实现AgentGUI，通过轨迹可视化、手动与自动干预机制，以及对多种开源和前沿代理框架的集成与协调，构建一个本地化、用户友好的图形界面系统。

Result: 用户研究表明，使用AgentGUI可显著缩短识别代理轨迹关键信息的时间（38%更快，p=0.023）；自动化漂移预防功能使小规模本地代理的任务完成率最高提升34个百分点（在0.8B–9B模型范围内，共50次运行）。

Conclusion: AgentGUI有效缓解了当前AI代理自主性增强与人类监控能力滞后之间的矛盾，为多代理系统的可观察性与可控性提供了实用解决方案，具备良好的可扩展性与开放性。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly adept at tackling complex, long-running tasks. With the rapid surge of autonomous capabilities, human oversight is systematically lagging behind due to limited human-centered interfacing. Aiming to address this, we introduce AgentGUI, a user-friendly, locally hosted GUI for seamlessly observing and steering AI agents amid multiple concurrent, long-running sessions. AgentGUI features 1) rich agent trajectory visualizations, 2) effective manual and automated steering, and 3) integration with and coordination between open-source and frontier agent frameworks. A controlled user study demonstrates statistically significant reduction in the time it takes to identify key elements from agent traces (38% faster, p = 0.023). In a preliminary experiment, AgentGUI's automated drift prevention feature raises the task completion rate of small local agents by as high as 34pp across a 0.8B--9B model ladder (N=50 runs per model). AgentGUI is publicly available through its project website (https://agent-gui-project.github.io) and open-source repository (https://github.com/eth-medical-ai-lab/agent-gui), along with a demo video (https://youtube.com/watch?v=GSDyxN1gTF0).

</details>


### [96] [When Synthetic Users Fail: A Cross-Domain Benchmark of LLM-Simulated Human Survey Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26348)
*Zihan Chen,Di Zhu,Lei Nico Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）作为合成用户在模拟人类回答方面的有效性，提出了一套评估智能合成用户系统的框架。通过在两个独立的人类数据集（美国社会态度调查和世界价值观调查）上测试四个不同规模和家族的LLM，发现所有模型在个体层面均无法超越非LLM基线，尤其在跨文化价值观方面差距显著且不受距离感知和正确评分机制缓解。此外，模型系统性地高估了人口统计学特征对态度的预测能力，这种偏差普遍存在且对编码方式不敏感。更大的模型也未能解决这些问题。决策影响分析显示，模型会将群体间差异放大2至4倍，导致目标定位错误，在美国和跨文化案例中分别有半数和多数情况下引导至错误群体，并虚构不存在的群体分裂。研究公开了跨域基准和评估框架，以帮助团队判断合成用户数据是否适用于决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型被广泛用作合成用户以支持产品、政策和市场决策，亟需评估其模拟人类响应的有效性与局限性，确保决策依据的可靠性。

Method: 采用统一协议在两个真实人类数据集上测试四个跨家族、跨规模的LLM，对比多种非LLM基线；通过人口统计学提示和调查模拟协议，分析个体层面表现、人口统计学过度预测问题及决策影响。

Result: 所有模型在个体层面均落后于最强基线，尤其在跨文化价值观任务中差距明显；普遍出现对人口统计学特征的过度依赖，且该偏差不随模型规模增大而改善；在实际应用中导致群体差异被严重夸大，引发错误的决策导向。

Conclusion: 当前的大型语言模型作为合成用户存在根本性缺陷，尤其在个体响应准确性和人口统计学偏差方面表现不佳。在缺乏充分验证前，不应将合成用户数据用于关键决策。建议使用本文提出的评估框架和跨域基准提前检验合成用户数据的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as synthetic users, stand-ins for human respondents whose simulated answers feed product, policy, and market decisions. We ask when this substitution is valid and when it fails, and package the answer as an evaluation framework for intelligent synthetic-user systems. A single protocol, run across four models spanning two families and an 8B-to-frontier capability range, is applied to two independent domains of real human-response data: U.S. general social attitudes (General Social Survey) and cross-cultural values (World Values Survey). Every model is benchmarked against a suite of non-LLM baselines fit on held-out human data. Under demographic prompting and the survey-simulation protocols we test, two failures replicate across both domains, all four models, and both families. First, at the individual level no LLM beats even the strongest baseline; on cross-cultural values every model falls well below it, and the gap survives distance-aware and proper scoring. Second, models systematically over-determine demographics, treating identity as far more predictive of attitudes than it is among real people, a distortion present for nearly every question-group combination and robust to a coding-invariant measure. Neither failure is remedied by a larger, more capable model. A decision-impact analysis shows why this matters in practice: on a segment-targeting task the models inflate between-segment gaps two to fourfold, would direct a team to the wrong segment in half of U.S. and most cross-cultural cases, and manufacture segment splits that do not exist in real people. We make the cross-domain benchmark and the evaluation framework available on request, so that teams can determine in advance when synthetic-user evidence is safe for decision support and when it is not.

</details>


### [97] [Symphony of Bias: Exploring Gender Associations with Musical Instruments in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26355)
*Farhan Farsi,Shayan Bali,Mohammad Heydari Rad,Negar Heidary,Donya Rooein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析大型语言模型（LLMs）对乐器的性别关联，揭示其在文本、视觉和音频模态中潜在的性别偏见。研究构建了多模态数据集Symphony-Bias，评估了10个不同架构和规模的多模态模型在22种乐器上的表现，发现92%的结果与社会科学研究中的性别刻板印象一致，其中竖琴和鼓的性别关联尤为显著。此外，模型在文本模态中性别偏见最强，视觉次之，音频最弱，表明模态特性会影响偏见的放大程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型广泛应用于信息获取，但可能延续社会偏见和强化性别刻板印象，因此有必要系统研究其在音乐乐器上的性别关联，以揭示潜在的偏见机制。

Method: 基于社会科学研究中的文化性别分类，构建包含文本、视觉和音频的多模态数据集Symphony-Bias，评估10个不同架构和规模的多模态模型在22种乐器上对男性、女性和非二元性别三类的关联性，跨模态分析性别偏见的表现。

Result: 92%的乐器层面结果与已有社会科学研究一致，竖琴和鼓表现出高度一致的性别关联；在三种模态中，文本模态的性别偏见最强，视觉次之，音频最弱，说明模态特性会显著影响偏见表达强度。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型在乐器性别关联上普遍存在与社会刻板印象一致的偏见，且偏见强度受模态类型影响，提示在设计和应用多模态模型时需关注模态特异性偏见风险，并采取相应缓解措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in everyday life and widely used for information seeking, raising concerns about their potential to perpetuate social biases and reinforce stereotypes. In this study, we investigate gender bias in LLMs through the lens of their associations with musical instruments. Building on social-science research on the cultural gender-typing of instruments, we introduce Symphony-Bias, a parallel multimodal dataset spanning text, vision, and audio. We evaluate ten multimodal models with diverse architectures and scales across 22 musical instruments, analyzing how they associate each instrument with three gender categories: {male, female, non-binary}, across three modalities: {text, vision, audio}. Our results show that 92\% of instrument-level outcomes align with prior social-science findings, with the harp and drums showing particularly consistent gendered associations across all evaluated models and modalities. We further find that alignment with social stereotypes is weakest in audio, stronger in vision, and strongest in text, suggesting that modality-specific representations can differentially amplify gendered associations with musical instruments.\footnote{The Symphony-Bias dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance of the paper.}

</details>


### [98] [Diagnosing Fine-Grained Inconsistency Classification in Financial Disclosure Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26368)
*Aman Kumar,Lasitha Vidyaratne,Dipanjan D Ghosh,Arnab Chakrabarti,Ahmed K Farahat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对财务披露中的细粒度不一致分类问题，基于SBID-FD基准数据集，比较了多种模型在不同设置下的表现。结果显示，微调的300M编码器达到61.9%准确率，与LoRA适配的Qwen3.5-9B和GPT-5.4性能相当，表明紧凑型监督编码器在实际效率上具有优势。提供黄金证据可提升至65.3%，自动预测证据仅部分恢复增益，说明证据定位仍是瓶颈。类别分析显示，指代性不一致对定位质量敏感，而事实性和逻辑性不一致即使有正确证据仍难区分，表明未来进展需同时改进证据提取与跨类别推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
