Conclusion: TREK 提供了一个严格、可复现、可审计的旅行规划评估基准，揭示了当前主流大模型在复杂多约束环境下生成真正可行行程的能力严重不足，尤其是在理解并响应用户隐含需求方面仍存在根本性缺陷。该基准已开源，可用于未来研究的标准化评测。

Abstract: Travel planning is a demanding stress test for tool-using LLM agents: a usable itinerary is a single artifact that must be right along many axes at once - every flight, hotel, and attraction must exist and be bookable, the days must be physically traversable, the total must clear a budget, and the plan must serve a traveler whose needs are only partly stated. Existing agent benchmarks reward these properties one at a time and grade the final output with soft or LLM-judged rubrics, which cannot certify that a returned plan is executable and are neither reproducible nor auditable. We introduce TREK (Travel Reasoning and Evaluation Kit), a benchmark for feasible itinerary synthesis: producing a single plan that is jointly constraint-correct, hallucination-free, spatio-temporally executable, budget-valid, and responsive to the traveler's unstated persona needs. TREK comprises 800 multi-constraint tasks - 533 feasible and 267 provably infeasible with typed route/entity/budget causes - over a synthetic, internally consistent knowledge base of 212,530 records across 375 cities and 13 personas, served through a production-style tool sandbox of validated RESTful APIs. Every task is scored by a fully deterministic, rule-based evaluator with no LLM judge and ships a human-verified gold reference that scores a perfect 1.0 under that same evaluator, so the ceiling is demonstrably achievable and every remaining gap is an agent limitation rather than scorer strictness. Evaluating 15 LLM agents across nine constraint dimensions, we find that even the strongest (GPT-5.6) produces a fully-feasible plan on only 46.2% of solvable tasks, with a median of 6.6% and a floor of 0.0%; satisfying travelers' unstated needs emerges as the universal bottleneck, unsolved even at the frontier. We release the dataset, tool sandbox, deterministic evaluator, and agent code as a fully reproducible benchmark.

</details>


### [131] [OptimismBench: Forecasting Bias and the Alignment Effect in Language Model Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26981)
*Seonglae Cho,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OptimismBench，一种通过反向配对检测大语言模型概率判断中系统性偏向的方法。该方法在无真实标签的情况下，通过比较同一情境下成功与失败的概率估计差异，揭示模型的乐观或悲观倾向。实验发现16个模型中有14个呈现乐观偏差，仅Anthropic的前沿模型为悲观；后训练显著影响偏差方向，且该模式在多种提示和参数设置下保持稳定。跨语言分析显示模型身份比语言影响更大，模型间差异是语言间差异的4.7倍。研究结果表明，对齐过程可能使模型更“助人”，但同时引入概率倾斜，下游应用默认继承此偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有校准指标无法捕捉概率判断中的系统性方向偏差，因它们只关注误差的绝对值而忽略符号。自然场景中缺乏真实概率作为参考，导致难以检测模型是否存在乐观或悲观倾向。本文旨在开发一种无需真实标签即可识别此类偏差的方法。

Method: 提出OptimismBench框架，利用成对的正反问题（如成功概率与失败概率）来评估同一事件的两种表述下的概率输出，通过两者的不对称性计算有符号的偏差得分，从而检测模型是否系统性偏向乐观或悲观。

Result: 在16个来自8家厂商的大模型中，14个表现出乐观偏差，仅Anthropic的高端模型为悲观；基线与对话模型对比显示后训练改变了偏差方向，不同模型家族表现不同；该偏差模式在提示、温度、视角及自校正操作下仍稳定存在；跨语言测试表明模型身份主导偏差，模型间差异是语言间差异的4.7倍。

Conclusion: 当对齐使模型更符合人类期望时，它也引入了概率上的系统性倾斜。这种倾斜被下游系统默认继承，因此必须对模型进行定向偏差审计，以保障决策公平性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as decision aids whose probability judgments shape downstream choices. Whether those judgments carry a systematic directional tilt has been hard to detect: calibration metrics aggregate unsigned errors, and naturalistic uncertainty offers no ground-truth probability. When an LLM rates a startup's success at 70% but its failure at 15%, the missing 15 points expose a distortion no aggregate score flags. We introduce OptimismBench, which detects directional bias with inverted pairs: each scenario elicits both P(success) and P(failure), and asymmetry between the two framings yields a signed bias score without ground truth. Across 16 models from 8 providers, fourteen are optimistic; pessimism appears only in Anthropic's frontier tier. Eleven matched base-versus-chat pairs across four families show post-training sets the sign of the bias, with opposite shifts in different families. The pattern survives prompt, temperature, perspective, and self-debiasing ablations. A seventeen-model six-language comparison further shows model identity dominates language, with inter-model variance at 4.7x inter-language variance. We release 3,870 items across 10 languages for per-model directional-bias auditing. When alignment makes a model more helpful, it also tilts its probabilities; downstream pipelines inherit the tilt by default.

</details>


### [132] [Evaluating Regional Bias in LLMs From Abstract Stereotype to Concrete Social Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27022)
*Jiayuan Di,Haoyi Yang,Yufei Luo,Jiahui Qu,Yiming Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出S2D框架，系统评估大语言模型（LLM）中从抽象刻板印象到具体社会决策的区域偏见。覆盖中国34个省级行政区，评估六种LLM在温暖度（亲和力与可信度）和能力（能力与智力）两个维度的刻板印象评分，以及教育、职业和社会互动中的配对选择任务。结果显示，不同地区在评分上存在显著差异，且模型间高度一致，尤其在能力和职业决策方面。这些模式与区域经济和数字发展指标相关，表现出混合的人类刻板印象特征，部分地区在一个维度得分高但另一个维度低。此外，偏见在中文和英文提示下保持稳定。总体表明，LLM中的区域偏见普遍存在、系统性强且具有实际后果，呼吁更注重区域差异的评估与缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多单独分析区域偏见在刻板印象或社会决策中的表现，缺乏对其结构和连锁影响的系统理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示从抽象刻板印象到具体决策的连续性偏见机制。

Method: 提出S2D框架，涵盖中国全部34个省级行政区域；使用刻板印象评分（温暖度与能力）及三类配对选择任务（教育、职业、社会互动）；评估六种大语言模型；分析结果与经济、数字发展指标的相关性；对比中英文提示下的稳定性。

Result: 各地区在温暖度与能力评分上存在显著差异，模型间一致性高，尤其在能力与职业决策方面；偏见模式与区域经济及数字发展水平相关；呈现混合人类刻板印象特征（如某些地区高能力但低温暖度）；在中英文提示下偏见模式基本稳定。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中的区域偏见是普遍、系统且具有实际后果的，应推动更具区域敏感性的评估与干预策略。

Abstract: Regional bias in large language models (LLMs) may shape both perceptions of regional groups and decisions about individuals from different regions. Yet existing studies often examine these manifestations separately, leaving their structure and consequences unclear. We introduce Stereotypes-to-Decisions (S2D), a systematic framework evaluating regional bias from abstract stereotypes to concrete social decisions. Covering all 34 provincial-level administrative regions of China, S2D evaluates six LLMs using stereotype ratings of Warmth (perceived friendliness and trustworthiness) and Competence (perceived capability and intelligence), along with paired-choice tasks across Education, Occupation, and Social Interaction. Results reveal substantial regional differences in regional scores, with considerable agreement across models, especially for Competence and Occupation decisions. Furthermore, these patterns are associated with regional economic and digital development indicators and display mixed human-like stereotypes, with some regions rated highly on one dimension but poorly on the other. They also remain largely stable across Chinese and English prompts. Overall, our findings show that regional bias in LLMs is prevalent, systematic, and consequential, motivating more regionally aware evaluation and mitigation.

</details>


### [133] [DenseOn with the LateOn: Fully Open Dense and Late-Interaction Models for Multilingual, Long-Context, and Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27178)
*Raphaël Sourty,Antoine Chaffin,Paulo Roberto Moura Junior,Amélie Chatelain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种开放的端到端检索模型训练方法，通过translate-train研究英语监督在多语言检索中的迁移效果。重建并整理了6.65亿个英文对比预训练对和188万个带难样本挖掘的监督微调对，训练出两个1.49亿参数的模型（DenseOn和LateOn），在BEIR基准上分别达到56.20和57.22的平均nDCG@10，创下该规模下的新纪录。进一步将英语数据翻译为八种语言，生成28亿跨语言样本，训练出3.07亿参数的mDenseOn和mLateOn模型。结果显示，尽管共享骨干网络、数据和目标，密集模型在翻译语言中表现良好但对未见语言/脚本退化，而晚交互模型更具泛化能力，表明基于词元级别的匹配可使translate-train从目标语言扩展策略转变为多语言泛化方法。所有模型、数据集和代码均公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前先进检索模型依赖封闭训练数据，导致可复现性差。本文旨在提供一种开放的端到端训练方案，并探究英语监督在多语言检索中的迁移能力，以解决可复现性和跨语言泛化问题。

Method: 从34个公开来源重构14亿条英文对比预训练对，筛选出6.65亿条高质量对；通过挖掘难负样本构建188万条监督微调对；训练两个1.49亿参数模型（DenseOn和LateOn）；将英语数据翻译为八种语言，生成28亿跨语言对，训练3.07亿参数的mDenseOn和mLateOn模型；对比不同模型在多语言环境下的表现。

Result: DenseOn和LateOn在BEIR上分别达到56.20和57.22的平均nDCG@10，刷新同规模模型记录；mDenseOn在翻译语言中表现良好但泛化能力有限；mLateOn在未见语言和脚本上表现更优，表明晚交互机制有助于跨语言泛化。

Conclusion: 通过translate-train实现的多语言训练中，晚交互模型相比密集模型具有更强的泛化能力，证明了基于词元级匹配的策略能够将translate-train从单一语言扩展转化为真正的多语言通用方法。研究结果支持开放数据与代码共享，推动可复现性与多语言检索发展。

Abstract: State-of-the-art retrieval models increasingly rely on closed training data, creating a reproducibility gap. We present an open end-to-end recipe for training retrieval models and study how English supervision transfers to multilingual retrieval through translate-train. We first reconstruct and curate 665M English contrastive pre-training pairs from 1.4B pairs across 34 public sources and build 1.88M supervised fine-tuning pairs with mined hard negatives. Training yields two 149M-parameter models: DenseOn, a single-vector dense model, and LateOn, a ColBERT-style late-interaction model. They achieve 56.20 and 57.22 average nDCG@10 on BEIR, respectively, setting new state-of-the-art results for this size class. We then translate the validated English data into eight languages, yielding 2.8B pairs with cross-lingual samples, and train mDenseOn and mLateOn, two 307M-parameter models built on mmBERT-base. Despite sharing their backbone, data, and objectives, their representations behave differently: the dense model is strong on English and translated languages but degrades outside translate-train support, whereas the late-interaction model generalizes better to unseen languages and scripts. This suggests that token-level matching turns translate-train from a target-language expansion strategy into a multilingual generalization recipe. We publicly release the models, datasets, and training code.

</details>


### [134] [Pangram 4 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27183)
*Ben Glickenhaus,Katherine Thai,Jenna Russell,Elyas Masrour,Yue Han,Max Spero,Bradley Emi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Pangram 4 is a state-of-the-art deep-learning model for AI text classification, achieving an AUROC of 0.9916 with extremely low false positive (0.0041%) and false negative (0.3396%) rates. It outperforms Pangram 3 in accuracy, out-of-distribution generalization, robustness to adversarial attacks, and fine-grained detection of AI-human co-authored text and interleaved AI assistance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To improve the accuracy, robustness, and granularity of AI-generated text detection, especially in complex scenarios involving mixed human-AI content and adversarial inputs.

Method: Advanced deep learning architecture with enhanced training strategies and fine-grained feature extraction mechanisms tailored for distinguishing subtle differences between AI and human-written text.

Result: Pangram 4 achieves an AUROC of 0.9916, with very low error rates and superior performance across multiple benchmarks, including out-of-distribution settings and adversarial conditions.

Conclusion: Pangram 4 represents a significant advancement in AI text detection, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance and strong practical applicability in diverse real-world domains.

Abstract: We present Pangram 4, the latest deep-learning-based AI-text classification model from Pangram Labs. We achieve an AUROC of 0.9916 with a false positive rate of 0.0041% and a false negative rate of 0.3396%. In addition to its increased overall accuracy compared with Pangram 3, Pangram 4 exhibits superior out-of-distribution generalization and robustness to adversarial attacks. Another novel contribution of Pangram 4 is its improved ability to distinguish fine-grained edits and mixed AI-human co-authored text. We demonstrate improvements to both boundary detection tasks and the detection of interleaved AI assistance. Finally, we report metrics on standard AI detection benchmarks showing that Pangram 4 achieves state-of-the-art performance on the AI text detection task across a wide variety of settings and domains.

</details>


### [135] [APEX-Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27189)
*Julien Benchek,Austin Bennett,Jasmin Kern,Ryan Stevens,Rene Sultan,Charis Ching,Hayley Popiel,Vaibhav Mittal,Felix Mercier,Brendan Foody,Bertie Vidgen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APEX-Accounting 是由 Mercor 与 Ramp 合作推出的基准测试，用于评估前沿模型在会计实际工作中的表现，涵盖对账、费用计提、交易记账和报告生成等任务。该基准包含 160 个任务，分为 10 个世界，每个世界包含会计系统及各类文件（如表格、PDF）。所有任务均由会计与簿记专家编写并制定评分标准。在九个前沿模型中，Claude-Fable-5 (Max) 表现最佳，平均标准@3 得分为 56.4%，其次是 Muse-Spark-1.1 (xHigh) 的 52.6%。但无模型在 Pass@8 上超过 2.6%，最高为 21.5%。实验发现增加上下文长度（从 1 到 50 令牌）时出现辛普森悖论：整体分数上升，但在固定预算下，模型使用更多令牌的任务得分反而下降。该基准为封闭式，可应请求对任意前沿模型进行排行榜评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大模型是否具备执行真实会计工作的能力，填补现有基准在专业财务任务上的空白。

Method: 构建包含真实会计场景的多世界基准数据集，任务由专家设计并制定评分标准；在多个前沿模型上进行评估，并分析不同上下文长度对性能的影响。

Result: Claude-Fable-5 (Max) 在平均标准@3 上表现最佳（56.4%），但所有模型在通过率（Pass@8）上均低于 2.6%；增加上下文长度虽提升整体表现，却出现辛普森悖论现象。

Conclusion: 当前大模型尚未具备可靠完成复杂会计任务的能力，尽管在部分指标上有所进展，但距离专业水平仍有显著差距。同时，上下文长度并非线性提升性能的因素，需谨慎设计评估机制。

Abstract: We introduce APEX-Accounting, a benchmark built by Mercor in partnership with Ramp, to assess whether frontier models can do the real work of accountants. Tasks include reconciling accounts, accruing expenses, posting transactions, and producing reports. The private eval set comprises 160 tasks, split across 10 worlds. Each world contains an accounting system, as well as spreadsheets, PDFs, and other files. Every task was authored and solved by experts in accounting and bookkeeping, who also wrote grading rubrics. Across nine frontier models, Claude-Fable-5 (Max) leads with 56.4% Mean Criteria@3, ahead of Muse-Spark-1.1 (xHigh) at 52.6%. No model scores more than 2.6% Pass^8 (GPT-5.6-Sol (Max+Pro)) and the highest Pass@8 is 21.5% (Muse-Spark-1.1 (xHigh)). We experiment with increasing the token budget from $1 to $50 and observe an instance of Simpson's paradox: scores increase as the token budget increases but within a given budget-constrained harness, scores are lower on tasks where the model spends more tokens. As APEX-Accounting is a closed benchmark, leaderboard evals can be run for any frontier model on request.

</details>


### [136] [Mental World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27201)
*Hao Fei,Yiran Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为心理世界建模（Mental World Modeling, MWM）的通用理论框架，将心理变量（如信念、意图、情感等）作为世界模型的核心组成部分，而非事后解释。MWM维护一个耦合的物理-心理世界状态，生成特定目标的局部观察，并模拟候选行为如何同时更新物理与心理状态。作者基于该框架构建了MENTIS，一个无需训练且完全可解释的基准模型，其流程包括状态解析、目标观察生成、动作分解、物理与心理状态联合演化以及分支价值评估。在涵盖文本、图像和视听故事的高质量人工构建数据集上，实验表明显式建模心理状态对预测人类决策至关重要。分析也揭示了当前心理世界建模的瓶颈。研究认为MWM是世界建模的下一阶段：从模拟物理场景转向模拟行动者的心智。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型仅关注物理世界的表征与演化（如‘是什么’‘在哪里’‘如何变化’），但人类行为由隐藏的心理状态驱动（如信念、欲望、意图、情感及社会规范认知）。若忽略这些心理因素，即使对物理场景理解正确，也无法准确预测人类行为。因此，需要一种能整合心理状态的世界模型。

Method: 提出MWM框架，其核心是维护一个耦合的物理-心理世界状态；通过目标特定的局部观测生成机制，模拟不同行为对物理与心理状态的联合影响；采用分步流程实现：状态解析、目标观察生成、动作分解、物理-心理状态联合转移、分支层级价值评估；构建训练-free、可解释的基准模型MENTIS以验证框架有效性。

Result: 在多模态情境决策数据集上的实验表明，显式建模心理状态显著提升对人类决策的预测能力；8个基于现代大语言模型的世界模型对比显示，仅依赖物理建模的系统表现较差；深入分析揭示了当前心理建模在表征、推理和一致性方面的局限性。

Conclusion: MWM代表了世界建模的演进方向——从单纯模拟物理世界转向模拟心智如何在其中行动。心理状态不应是事后附加的解释，而应成为世界模型的内在核心。未来工作需解决心理状态的动态建模、可扩展性与跨情境泛化问题。

Abstract: World models enable a predictive substrate for planning and action, yet existing formulations merely answer a physical question: what/where it is, and how will it evolve. Human behavior, however, is driven by hidden mental state (what a person believes, wants, intends, feels, and considers socially permissible), so a model that tracks the physical scene but not what each agent knows and believes about it predicts the wrong action for the right-looking scene. We formulate Mental World Modeling (MWM), a generic theoretical framework that makes mental variables core components of a world model rather than posthoc rationales: MWM aintains a coupled physical-mental world state, renders a target-specific partial observation, and simulates how candidate actions jointly update both components. We instantiate the framework in MENTIS, a training-free and fully inspectable baseline that decomposes the process into state parsing, target-observation generation, action decomposition, coupled physical and mental transition, and branch-level value evaluation. On a manually constructed, quality-controlled dataset of situated decision scenarios spanning text, image, and sounding-video stories, experiments with 8 modern LLM-based world models demonstrate that explicitly modeling the mental state is essential for predicting human decisions. Deeper analyses further expose the bottlenecks of current mental world modeling. We expect MWM as a next stage of world modeling, from simulating physical scenes to simulating the minds that act in them.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Emergent Sparsity in Frozen Random CNN Feature Extractors for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26059)
*Scott M. Norton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度强化学习代理在使用固定随机初始化的CNN特征提取器时，自发产生了极稀疏的全连接表示，即使没有显式的稀疏性目标。在Pong任务中，第一层全连接层（FC1）仅需1-3个神经元即可压缩关键信息，而可训练的CNN则激活55-64个神经元。任务复杂度越高，稀疏性越明显：Pong为1-11，Breakout为19-26，Space Invaders约为42。稀疏性与任务结构相关，而非固定容量比例。同一游戏不同种子产生不同活性神经元数量（如5、7、11），且活性数较少的模型性能受限。移除活跃神经元会显著降低性能，证明其必要性。活性神经元集合在15-30M步内锁定，而奖励在35-105M步后才开始上升。在Breakout中，固定和可训练的CNN通过不同结构实现类似性能：前者使用17-25个活跃神经元，后者使用51个。当输入维度远大于任务内在维度时，梯度下降在固定随机投影上可揭示问题的有效秩，无需额外稀疏机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深度强化学习中在无显式稀疏性目标下，全连接层为何会出现极端稀疏表示；理解稀疏性与任务复杂度、随机投影结构及性能上限之间的关系。

Method: 使用固定随机初始化的CNN作为特征提取器，训练PPO代理于Pong、Breakout、Space Invaders等游戏；分析第一层全连接层（FC1）的活性神经元数量；进行宽度缩放实验、同一游戏不同种子对比、神经元移除消融实验、训练过程时间序列分析；比较固定与可训练CNN在Breakout中的瓶颈结构差异。

Result: FC1层表现出显著稀疏性，活性神经元数随任务复杂度增加而上升；同一任务不同种子导致不同活性数，且活性数影响最终性能；移除活跃神经元导致性能崩溃；活性集合在早期锁定；固定与可训练CNN通过不同结构实现相似性能；固定随机投影能揭示任务有效秩。

Conclusion: 在输入维度远大于任务内在维度的情况下，基于固定随机投影的梯度下降能自动揭示问题的有效秩，无需显式稀疏性设计。这种稀疏性是任务结构与随机投影共同作用的结果，对理解深度强化学习中的表征学习具有重要意义。

Abstract: We report a striking phenomenon: deep reinforcement learning agents trained with frozen, randomly initialized CNN feature extractors spontaneously develop extremely sparse fully-connected representations, without any sparsity-inducing objective. In the first fully-connected layer (FC1, $3{,}136 \to 64$), agents compress task-relevant information through as few as 1-3 neurons out of 64 for deterministic Pong (5-11 for stochastic Pong), while trainable CNNs activate 55-64 neurons under matched conditions. We establish four principal findings. First, FC1 sparsity scales with task complexity: 1-11 for Pong, 19-26 for Breakout, and $\sim$42 for Space Invaders. Width-scaling confirms this reflects task structure rather than a fixed capacity fraction. Second, within-game scaling emerges: three identical Pong seeds produce 5, 7, and 11 active neurons. The 5-neuron seed plateaus at $+14$ reward, while the others reach expert performance ($+18.4$, $+18.7$), suggesting the random projection's usable dimensionality bounds achievable performance. Third, ablation confirms necessity: removing these active neurons crashes performance across two PPO implementations and four games. Fourth, the information bottleneck commits early: a sweep shows the active set locks by 15-30M steps, while reward turns positive 35-105M steps later. A complementary finding in Breakout shows frozen and trainable CNNs reach competitive rewards via structurally different bottlenecks: frozen agents use 17-25 active neurons (participation ratio $\sim$10-14), while trainable agents use 51 (participation ratio $\sim$3.6). Finally, wherever input dimensionality dwarfs intrinsic task dimensionality, gradient descent on a frozen random projection may reveal the effective rank of the underlying problem without explicit sparsity machinery.

</details>


### [138] [Sim2Win: A Team-Agnostic, Event-Based Pre-Match Outcome Prediction and Tactical Profiling System for Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26061)
*Mouad Zemzoumi,Amine Abouaomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sim2Win提出了一种无队名、基于事件的赛前战术推荐框架，通过构建滚动战术档案、设计可解释的战术特征比率、聚类出8种踢球风格，并使用13个分类器预测比赛结果。该系统在未见球队上表现优异，平均ROC-AUC达0.704，准确率55.4%，优于ELO、Pi-Rating和GAP等基线模型，在21次比较中全部胜出ROC-AUC，19次胜出准确率。CatBoost在分布内表现最佳，准确率达60.90%。研究证明行为性战术表征具有跨分布转移的预测能力，可替代依赖身份信息的传统足球预测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统足球赛前战术决策依赖主观专家分析和基于身份的球探系统，难以泛化到未见过的球队。需要一种不依赖团队名称或身份特征、能通用且可解释的战术推荐框架。

Method: 利用StatsBomb公开事件数据构建五场比赛滚动战术档案；工程化四个可解释的战术特征比率；采用K-Means聚类将球队行为划分为八种踢球风格；训练十三个分类器，基于战术匹配表示预测胜/平/负概率。整个系统不使用团队名称或身份特征，实现对未见团队的泛化。

Result: 在留一竞赛外（LOCO）评估中，Sim2Win在完全未见的球队上取得平均ROC-AUC 0.704，平均准确率55.4%；在21次ROC-AUC比较中全面超越基线模型，在19次准确率比较中领先；其中CatBoost在分布内表现最佳，准确率60.90%。

Conclusion: 行为性战术表征在分布变化下仍具备可转移的预测信号，表明其可作为依赖身份信息的足球预测系统的有效替代方案，推动战术决策向数据驱动与泛化能力更强的方向发展。

Abstract: Pre-match tactical decision-making in professional football relies heavily on subjective expert analysis and identity-based scouting systems that cannot generalize to unseen teams. This paper presents Sim2Win, a team-agnostic, event-based pre-match tactical recommendation framework that reframes match outcome prediction as a tactical decision-support problem. Using StatsBomb open event data from eleven competitions spanning 178 teams and 1,411 team-match records, Sim2Win constructs five-match rolling tactical profiles, engineers four interpretable tactical feature ratios, clusters team behaviors into eight playstyles via K-Means, and trains thirteen classifiers to estimate win, draw, and loss probabilities from tactical matchup representations.
  The system operates without team names or identity features, enabling generalization to teams never seen during training. A rigorous Leave-One-Competition-Out (LOCO) evaluation demonstrates that Sim2Win achieves a mean ROC-AUC of 0.704 and mean accuracy of 55.4% on completely unseen teams, outperforming ELO, Pi-Rating, and GAP baselines on all 21 ROC-AUC comparisons and 19 of 21 accuracy comparisons. Among all evaluated models, CatBoost achieved the strongest in-distribution performance with 60.90% accuracy.
  These findings suggest that behavioral tactical representations provide transferable predictive signal under distribution shift and offer a viable alternative to identity-dependent football prediction systems.

</details>


### [139] [Meta-Learned Reward Shaping for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26094)
*Yunpeng Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MeRLa提出了一种基于元学习的奖励塑形框架，通过在辅助任务上预训练任务感知的奖励塑形函数，提升强化学习从人类反馈（RLHF）中对大语言模型的对齐效果。该方法在保持策略最优性的同时提供任务特定的学习信号，结合任务区分、熵正则化和基于势能的保守性，实现稳定收敛。实验表明，在多个基准测试中优于PPO、DPO、GRPO和DAPO，显著提升性能并降低41%的训练不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的强化学习从人类反馈（RLHF）受限于静态且任务无关的奖励模型，导致学习信号稀疏、对齐效果不佳。需要一种能够根据具体任务动态调整奖励信号的方法，以提高对齐质量与训练稳定性。

Method: 提出MeRLa框架，利用元学习在多个辅助任务上训练一个任务感知的奖励塑形函数Φ(x,y;φ)，该函数生成复合奖励，结合任务区分性、熵正则化与潜在函数保守性，确保策略不变性并缓解激励错位问题。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上进行的实验显示，MeRLa在AlpacaEval 2.0上达到90.8%的长度控制胜率，在MT-Bench上得分为9.14，训练不稳定性降低41%，且在与过程和评分增强奖励结合时仍保持优势。

Conclusion: MeRLa通过元学习构建任务感知的奖励塑形机制，有效解决了传统RLHF中奖励信号稀疏与任务无关的问题，显著提升了对齐性能与训练稳定性，为大语言模型对齐提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard approach for aligning large language models with human preferences, but its quality is limited by static, task-agnostic reward models. This mismatch leads to sparse learning signals and suboptimal alignment. We introduce MeRLa (Meta-Learned Reward Shaping), a principled framework that meta-learns a task-aware shaping function $Φ(x,y;φ)$ across auxiliary tasks before RLHF training. The learned shaping produces a composite reward that preserves policy optimality while providing task-specific learning signals. Our meta-objective combines task discrimination, entropy regularization, and potential-based conservation for stable convergence. We provide theoretical guarantees for policy invariance, analyze representation drift sensitivity, and formally address incentive misalignment from entropy maximization. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B across four benchmarks show consistent improvements over PPO, DPO, GRPO, and DAPO, achieving a 90.8% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0 and a score of 9.14 on MT-Bench, with 41% less training instability. MeRLa retains its benefits when combined with process-based and rubric-based enhanced rewards.

</details>


### [140] [Data Fusion and Contrastive Alignment for Unconstrained IR Molecular Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26164)
*Ethan J. Mick,Campbell A. Sweet,Matthias J. Young,Derek T. Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出改进的基于Transformer的编码器-解码器模型，通过引入混合专家（MoE）解码器模块和非加性聚合方法（如线性顺序统计与Choquet积分），提升红外光谱数据下分子结构的无约束预测能力。结合对比对齐损失，显著提高Top-K预测准确率超10个百分点，并验证了红外光谱包含绝大多数化学信息，推动AI在分析化学中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化分子结构解析方法依赖预设化学式，仅能识别异构体而非完整结构；尽管Transformer模型在异构体识别中表现良好，但在无约束结构预测中可靠性不足且机制不明确。因此亟需改进模型以突破化学空间的限制。

Method: 采用新型混合专家（MoE）解码器，利用非加性聚合方式（线性顺序统计与Choquet积分）处理谱图表示；同时在编码器-解码器结构中引入非加性操作，增强对复杂化学空间的建模能力；并加入辅助对比对齐损失项优化特征学习。

Result: 相较于基线仅使用红外光谱的模型，本方法在Top-K预测准确率上提升超过10个百分点；子结构片段分析表明红外光谱已蕴含绝大部分关键化学信息，支持模型性能提升主要源于对吸收带重叠或未充分代表分子的捕捉。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了仅凭红外光谱即可实现高效且准确的自动化分子结构解析，显著拓展了人工智能在分析化学领域的应用边界。

Abstract: Automated molecular structure elucidation from infrared (IR) spectroscopy data has seen significant advancements in recent years, but its broad applicability is limited by a reliance on pre-determined chemical formulas provided as auxiliary model inputs. This limits model predictions to isomer identification rather than full molecular structure prediction. Although transformer models have been shown to identify molecular isomers with high accuracy, their reliability for unconstrained structure elucidation is comparatively low and poorly understood. In this work, we propose and evaluate key modifications to the traditional encoder-decoder transformer. To better address the vast chemical space of the unconstrained problem, we implement a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder module that utilizes non-additive aggregation via linear-order statistics and the Choquet integral. We further modify the transformer to utilize these non-additive operators when aggregating spectral representations as well. Together with an auxiliary contrastive alignment loss term, these enhancements improve Top-K prediction accuracy by over 10 percentage points compared to baseline IR-only models. Through sub-structure fragment analysis of molecular predictions, we further confirm that infrared spectra encode the vast majority of relevant chemical information, implying that the higher performance of isomer-ranking models is largely due to underrepresented or overlapping absorption bands for molecules in the explored chemical space. Ultimately, by demonstrating the efficacy of automated molecular structure elucidation from measured IR spectra, this work serves to significantly broaden the utility of AI in analytical chemistry.

</details>


### [141] [Shared SFT Lessons Across Alignment, Model Organisms, and Toy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26173)
*Anton de la Fuente,Arthur Conmy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了对齐训练、模型生物和玩具模型三个研究领域中监督微调（SFT）的共性，并通过三个跨领域知识迁移实验验证了不同领域间可转移的经验。首先，将对齐训练中关于行为泛化的方法（如解释行为原因）引入玩具模型，提升泛化能力；其次，从模型生物研究中借鉴能力保持策略，使用非学生模型生成的数据进行SFT时，混合良性同模型数据可有效防止能力损失；最后，揭示在对齐设置中仅保持能力并不足以保证鲁棒性，后续良性SFT可能破坏对齐行为。研究证明跨领域借鉴SFT经验能促进各领域发展，呼吁更多研究人员打破领域壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对齐训练、模型生物和玩具模型被视为独立研究领域，但它们常使用监督微调（SFT）实现相似目标。当多个领域共享目标时，应探索其经验是否可迁移。本文旨在检验不同领域中已验证的SFT策略能否在其他领域中发挥作用，以推动各领域共同进步。

Method: 通过三组跨领域实验，分别将对齐训练中的行为泛化方法、模型生物中的能力保持策略以及模型生物中的鲁棒性启示应用于其他领域。具体包括：1）在玩具模型中测试基于“行为原因”的SFT是否提升泛化性能；2）在模型-特定中期对齐设置中，评估使用非学生模型输出与同模型数据混合训练的效果；3）考察后续良性SFT对已对齐行为的影响，检验能力保持与鲁棒性的关系。

Result: 1）在玩具模型中，基于行为原因的训练显著提升了行为泛化能力；2）混合使用非学生模型输出与同模型数据，可在保留能力的同时有效嵌入目标行为；3）即使能力得以保持，后续良性微调仍可能导致对齐行为被消除，说明能力保持不等于鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移监督微调经验具有显著价值，能够提升不同研究领域的效率与效果。未来应鼓励研究者打破学科壁垒，积极借鉴其他领域的成熟技术，从而推动整体人工智能研究的发展。

Abstract: Alignment training, model organisms, and toy models are usually treated as separate research areas. But projects in all three frequently use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to pursue the same underlying goals. When projects share a goal, we should test whether lessons learned from one area transfer to the other areas. We study three such transfers, each taking a lesson developed in one SFT setting and testing it in another. First, we port a lesson about behavior generalization from alignment training into toy models. Training on the reason for a behavior, as in Teaching Claude Why, can make the behavior generalize better than training on examples of the behavior alone. Second, we port a lesson about capability preservation from model organisms into the Model-Spec Midtraining alignment setting. SFT on outputs written by a model other than the student (off-model outputs) can damage capabilities when trained on. Mixing in benign on-model (and on-policy) data into our training can prevent most of this damage while still embedding the target behavior. Third, we port a lesson about robustness from model organisms into the same alignment setting. We find that follow-up benign SFT can erase the alignment behavior while preserving capabilities, showing that capability preservation alone does not ensure robustness to subsequent training. Our work illustrates how porting SFT lessons between different research fields can uplift them all, suggesting more researchers should borrow techniques from outside their own areas.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic Parameterization Is Not Dynamic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26192)
*Zongfei Li,Yuan-yih Shang,Guozhong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了输入依赖的控制器系数在动态推理或计算节省中的解释问题，指出这种解释混淆了系数变化、冻结模型对系数分配的依赖性以及条件执行三个概念。研究聚焦于第二种性质，提出了一种通用的冻结控制器审计原则，并实现为FCA方法：通过缓存未扰动轨迹上的完整系数张量，禁用控制器，再以跨输入重分配、词元打乱和独立校准集估计的静态配置重新播放冻结模型。由于系数在干预前已缓存，性能变化可准确衡量系数分配依赖性而无反馈影响。在七个76M FeatureGate Transformer和三个504M模型中，静态分层配置保留了98.70%和99.43%的正确率相对于全局均值的性能差距；层身份解释了87%至96%的系数方差。尽管如此，FeatureGate仍执行每个Transformer块，其测量推理速度比密集模型慢30.8%。在公开的MUDDPythia-1.4B检查点上，跨输入重分配和词元打乱分别导致NLL增加1.9067和2.9637，表明模型强烈依赖于内容条件的跨层分配。结果表明，仅凭动态参数化不能证明动态推理，功能动态也不代表计算节省。关于动态模型的声明应分别报告系数变化、冻结模型的功能依赖性和实际执行情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对输入依赖控制器系数的解释常被用于支持动态推理或计算节省的观点，但这种解释可能混淆了系数变化、冻结模型对系数分配的依赖性以及条件执行三者之间的区别。尤其在缺乏严格评估的情况下，容易误判模型是否真正实现了动态推理或高效计算。因此需要一种系统性的方法来准确评估冻结控制器的依赖性，从而澄清这些概念。

Method: 提出冻结控制器审计（FCA）方法：首先在未扰动轨迹上缓存完整的系数张量；随后禁用控制器；再通过跨输入重新分配、词元打乱和基于独立校准集的静态配置进行重播。该方法确保性能变化仅反映系数分配依赖性，而不受控制器在扰动隐藏状态下的反馈影响。

Result: 在7个76M FeatureGate Transformer和3个504M模型中，静态分层配置分别保留了98.70%和99.43%的正确率-全局均值性能差距；层身份解释了87%至96%的系数方差。在MUDDPythia-1.4B上，跨输入重分配和词元打乱使NLL分别上升1.9067和2.9637，表明模型高度依赖内容条件的跨层系数分配。然而，所有模型仍执行全部Transformer块，且推理速度比密集模型慢30.8%。

Conclusion: 动态参数化本身不足以证明动态推理或计算节省。必须明确区分并报告系数变化、冻结模型的功能依赖性以及实际执行行为，才能准确评估动态模型的有效性。

Abstract: Input-dependent controller coefficients are often treated as evidence of dynamic inference or computational savings. This interpretation conflates three properties: coefficient variation, dependence of a frozen model on how coefficients are assigned to inputs, and conditional execution. We focus on the second property and formulate a general principle of frozen-controller auditing. We provide one concrete implementation, Frozen-Controller Auditing (FCA), which caches the complete coefficient tensor along an unperturbed trajectory, disables the controller, and replays the frozen model with cross-input reassignment, token shuffling, and static profiles estimated from an independent calibration set. Because the coefficients are cached before any intervention, performance changes under replay measure assignment dependence without feedback from recomputing the controller on perturbed hidden states. Across seven independently trained 76M FeatureGate Transformers and three 504M models, static layerwise profiles retain 98.70% and 99.43% of the Correct-to-GlobalMean performance gap, respectively. Layer identity explains 87% to 96% of the coefficient variance. FeatureGate nevertheless executes every Transformer block, and its measured inference is 30.8% slower than Dense. On the public MUDDPythia-1.4B checkpoint, cross-input reassignment and token shuffling increase NLL by 1.9067 and 2.9637, respectively. These penalties show that the model depends strongly on content-conditioned cross-layer assignment. MUDDPythia also executes every Transformer block. The results show that dynamic parameterization alone does not establish dynamic inference and that functional dynamics do not establish computational savings. Claims about dynamic models should separately report coefficient variation, functional dependence of the frozen model, and actual execution.

</details>


### [143] [Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26246)
*Fangxu Yu,Zinan Lin,Xiaodong Liu,Weijia Xu,Michael Xu,Tianyi Zhou,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: W2S-OPD 是一种新的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过从多个弱模型中蒸馏能力来提升强学生模型，无需依赖更强的教师模型。它利用对比对（如预强化学习与后强化学习模型、不同规模的基础模型、正确与错误提示）在logit空间构建代理教师，提取关键能力方向并注入学生模型，从而在数学和代码任务上超越传统方法，甚至超过原始教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于策略的蒸馏方法依赖于比学生更强的教师模型，但在模型能力边界处无法应用；同时，训练大型教师模型成本高昂。为解决这一问题，本文提出一种从弱模型中提炼强能力的新范式，以突破教师模型能力限制。

Method: W2S-OPD 构建一个代理教师，通过对比一对较弱但低成本的模型（正例与负例）在logit空间中的差异，提取出能力方向，并将其添加到学生模型的基础上形成分布接近的学生自身基线。随后，学生在自己的采样轨迹上最小化反向KL散度，实现对代理教师的蒸馏。

Result: 在四个数学和三个代码基准测试中，W2S-OPD 均优于传统OPD方法，使学生模型性能超越领域教师，且即使所有监督源均弱于学生，仍能持续提升性能。不同对比方式提供不同信号：强化学习对比强调推理框架，规模对比强调求解过程，提示对比强调实例级解题方向。

Conclusion: W2S-OPD 成功实现了从弱模型中挖掘强能力，拓展了在线策略蒸馏的应用边界，尤其适用于无强大教师可用或训练成本受限的场景。该方法具有可扩展性和灵活性，为大模型能力迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD), which aligns a student with the teacher's token-level distribution on the student's own rollouts, is an effective paradigm for transferring capabilities across LLMs. Prevailing approaches assume a teacher at least as capable as the student: they either distill a larger model into a smaller one, which fails at the frontier where no larger teacher exists, or consolidate multiple domain experts trained from a shared base, which requires costly training at the student's scale. We introduce Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD), a simple yet effective OPD framework that improves the strong student by distilling from multiple weak models. W2S-OPD constructs a proxy teacher in logit space from a contrast pair of a positive and a negative model, both smaller than the student and cheap to obtain. Their logit difference isolates the capability direction, which is added to the student's own base model, yielding a proxy teacher that couples this direction while staying distributionally adjacent to the student. The student then distills it by minimizing the per-token reverse KL on its own rollouts. We instantiate the contrast pair as i) a post-RL expert against its pre-RL initialization, isolating the skill RL instills, ii) a larger against a smaller base model, isolating the capability from scale, and iii) a small base model with correct versus wrong hints, isolating the instance-level direction toward the solution. Across four math and three code benchmarks, W2S-OPD outperforms OPD, enables the student to surpass the domain teacher, and keeps improving the student even when every supervision source is weaker. Analysis shows different contrasts yield distinct signals: the post-RL and hint contrasts emphasize reasoning frameworks, while the scale contrast emphasizes the solving procedure. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yu-Fangxu/W2S-OPD.

</details>


### [144] [Between Gradient and Natural Gradient: A Continuum of LoRA Initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26247)
*Dianze Liu,Farshid Ghezelbash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一的LoRA初始化方法（ULoRA），通过两个参数控制谱白化和对角线预处理，形成一个连续的优化空间。实验表明最佳配置任务依赖且常位于该空间内部，而非固定端点。优化后的ULoRA在多个基准测试中表现媲美甚至超越全量微调；其无需搜索的变体ULoRA-Auto基于层内统计自动选择参数，性能接近上界，是当前部署友好型LoRA方法中的佼佼者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化方法（如基于梯度投影或损失曲率白化）看似不同，实则可统一为一种更通用的预处理框架。当前方法多采用固定策略，缺乏对最优初始化条件的系统探索，限制了性能潜力。

Method: 提出统一的两参数预处理框架ULoRA，包含谱白化指数与Adam式对角指数，将多种现有初始化方法视为其特例。通过全学习率搜索遍历该连续空间，结合任务特性寻找最优配置，并设计无搜索需求的ULoRA-Auto变体，依据每层的谱统计信息自动选择参数。

Result: ULoRA在五个GLUE任务上使用RoBERTa-base达到或超过全量微调性能，在GSM8K任务上与最强基线相当；ULoRA-Auto无需额外搜索成本即可逼近最优性能，成为部署友好的顶尖LoRA方法之一。

Conclusion: LoRA的初始化与曲率预处理应被视为可调参数而非固定设计。通过系统性地探索其连续设计空间，可显著提升性能，且可通过自适应机制实现高效部署。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tunes large pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but its performance depends strongly on how the adapters are initialized. Recent schemes initialize the adapters from the downstream loss gradient: some project the raw gradient onto its top directions, while others first whiten it with an estimate of the loss curvature. We show that these seemingly distinct methods are points on a single continuum: a two-parameter family of preconditioned gradient initializations, which we call Unified LoRA (ULoRA), governed by a spectral whitening exponent and an Adam-like diagonal exponent. Sweeping this family under a full learning-rate search, we find that no single fixed preconditioning strength dominates: the best operating point is task-dependent and frequently lies strictly inside the family, away from the published endpoints. Treated as an upper bound of this family, a tuned ULoRA configuration matches or exceeds full fine-tuning on all five GLUE tasks with RoBERTa-base and is competitive with the strongest baselines on GSM8K with LLaMA-2-7B. Our deployable, search-free variant, ULoRA-Auto, selects per-layer exponents from measured spectral statistics, approaches this upper bound at no additional search cost, and ranks at or near the top among deployable LoRA methods. Our results show that a principled design space for LoRA initialization and curvature preconditioning should be treated as a tunable dimension rather than a fixed design decision.

</details>


### [145] [Early Verdicts, Better Budgets: Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation for Compute-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26253)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SARA提出一种基于序贯自适应采样策略的强化学习方法，通过在早期阶段判断提示的有效性，有效减少无效滚动开销，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在生成回滚时面临效率瓶颈，尤其当大量提示产生饱和组（全正确或全错误）时，零奖励方差导致无法获得策略梯度信号。传统方法要么过度采样并丢弃饱和提示（动态采样），增加额外开销；要么提前预测提示难度，但对策略变化敏感且不稳定。

Method: 将每步回滚收集建模为预算约束下的序贯分配问题，引入SARA（Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation）。SARA维护每个提示成功概率的贝塔后验分布，使用闭式预测器评估组的有效性，并采用双阈值SPRT风格规则：确认有效组、短探查后放弃已确定的饱和组，并将释放的预算重新分配给新提示，无需额外预测回滚。

Result: 在1.5B/3B模型上进行数学推理与规划任务时，SARA在单个GPU上达到与DPS相当的性能（低于DS基线），但仅需22%更少的回滚次数；结合SARA与DPS可实现最佳准确率，略高于DS，同时减少67%的回滚开销（接近均匀成本）。

Conclusion: SARA通过早期有效性判断和预算智能重分配，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低强化学习中的回滚成本，为高效验证奖励机制提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is bottlenecked by rollout generation, yet many sampled prompts produce saturated groups (all responses correct or all incorrect) whose zero reward variance yields no policy-gradient signal. Existing remedies either oversample a larger candidate pool and discard saturated prompts (dynamic sampling), paying heavy extra rollouts, or predict prompt difficulty before sampling, which is fragile under a shifting policy. We observe that a group's effectiveness is usually decided early, within the first few of its rollouts, so spending a full group on an already-decided prompt is wasteful. We cast per-step rollout collection as a budget-constrained sequential allocation (optimal stopping) problem and introduce SARA (Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation). SARA maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success rate, evaluates a closed-form predictor of group effectiveness, and applies a two-threshold, SPRT-style rule that commits effective groups, abandons saturated ones after a short probe, and reallocates the freed budget to fresh prompts, without any extra prediction rollouts. We prove abandonment reliability, expected rollout savings, fixed-budget yield dominance, and a link between effective-group yield and the GRPO gradient norm. On mathematical reasoning and planning with 1.5B/3B models on a single GPU, SARA matches DPS (both below the DS oracle) while using 22% fewer rollouts than DS; composing SARA with DPS yields the best accuracy, slightly above DS, at 67% fewer rollouts (near-uniform cost).

</details>


### [146] [Top-$k$ Pareto Bandits: Hypervolume Regret for Multi-Objective Slate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26273)
*Nicolas Gutowski,Fabien Chhel,Alexandre Letard,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在半赌徒反馈下，通过选择大小为 $k$ 的臂集合来近似多目标奖励向量的帕累托前沿的随机多目标老虎机问题。作者提出 \textit{THV-UCB} 算法，基于对边际超体积贡献的乐观估计进行贪心选择，并建立了无间隙和有间隙的后悔界，证明了该方法在理论上可有效逼近帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标决策中，传统方法往往寻找单一最优解，但实际应用中更关注一组能近似帕累托前沿的优质解。本文旨在通过小规模动作集近似帕累托前沿，以提高算法效率与实用性。

Method: 提出 \textit{THV-UCB} 算法，利用对每个臂的边际超体积贡献的乐观估计进行贪心选择，从而最大化所选集合的支配超体积。

Result: 建立了 $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ 的无间隙后悔界和 $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ 的间隙依赖后悔界，表明算法在不同条件下均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用小规模子集近似多目标问题中的帕累托前沿提供了理论支持，适用于多种多目标应用场景。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic multi-objective bandit problem where, at each round, the agent selects a slate of $k$ arms and observes their $d$-dimensional reward vectors under semi-bandit feedback. We do not aim at identifying a single optimal arm; instead, we consider the problem of maintaining a small set of actions that jointly approximate the Pareto frontier. We formalize this objective through the dominated hypervolume induced by the selected subset of arms, and define an $α$-approximate hypervolume regret with respect to the best size-$k$ subset achievable in hindsight, where $α= 1 - 1/e$ reflects the approximation guarantee of greedy maximization for monotone submodular functions. To address this problem, we introduce \textit{THV-UCB}, an optimistic algorithm that selects arms greedily based on optimistic estimates of their marginal hypervolume contributions. We establish a gap-free regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ that holds on every instance, together with a gap-dependent bound $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ that becomes polylogarithmic in $T$ once the arms are sufficiently well separated. Our results provide theoretical support for using small subsets to approximate Pareto fronts in various multi-objective applications.

</details>


### [147] [FloDR: An invertible dimensionality reduction method based on a normalising flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26278)
*Abdallah Baraka,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FloDR 是一种基于可逆归一化流的降维方法，通过保留高维嵌入中的额外坐标，提供更丰富的信息。与 t-SNE 和 UMAP 不同，它不仅生成二维可视化，还具备精确的逆映射和密度估计能力，支持诊断性可视化，如条件扩散和隐藏对比度场，从而揭示数据结构中被传统方法忽略的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降维方法（如 t-SNE、UMAP）在将高维数据投影到二维时会丢弃大量信息，导致距离、簇间关系、空隙意义及隐藏结构难以解读。需要一种能保留更多信息并支持精确反演与评估的方法。

Method: FloDR 使用可逆归一化流进行降维，仅用前两个输出坐标生成二维嵌入，但保留其余坐标。训练后可获得精确逆映射与密度估计，进而计算条件扩散（衡量输入空间不确定性）和隐藏对比度（衡量标签对比信息的丢失程度），并通过留出集和自助法置信区间进行统计检验。

Result: FloDR 能够在二维嵌入中呈现传统方法无法捕捉的结构信息；条件扩散与隐藏对比度场提供了对嵌入可靠性的定量评估，且可通过统计检验识别不可靠区域（报告为拒绝）。

Conclusion: FloDR 通过保留完整高维表示并利用可逆模型的数学性质，实现了更可解释、可验证的降维可视化，显著提升了对嵌入结果的理解深度。

Abstract: It is common for two-dimensional embeddings of high-dimensional data to be read far beyond what they can support. Distances in and between clusters, the meaning behind empty spaces, and the amount of structure hidden at each point are generally invisible in the output of methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. This is because the information that could support the meaning of these properties is discarded during the optimisation process. Here, we present FloDR, a dimensionality reduction method that embeds data through an invertible normalising flow. While FloDR only uses the first two output coordinates to create a two-dimensional embedding, it retains the remaining coordinates rather than discarding them. In addition to the embedding, an exact inverse and an exact density are properties of a trained mapping, which enable diagnostic visualisations that are computed from the exact inverse of the model that drew the layout rather than from an approximate one. Specifically, we draw two fields, the conditional spread, which measures how much of the original data remains undetermined at each embedding position in input units, and the hidden contrast, which measures how much information about a labelled contrast the two plotted coordinates discard. Both fields are rendered with a prespecified test against a held out portion of the input data and a bootstrap confidence. A field that fails the test is reported as refused.

</details>


### [148] [Entity Resolution in Practice: Lessons from a Self-Serve Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26298)
*Kaushik Pavani,Ganga Aluri,Pravin Jadhav,Neeraj Prasad,Kiran Sanka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建并评估了一个自服务实体消歧（ER）系统，在六个涵盖864至500万条记录的基准测试上进行了实验，总结出三个现有文献中缺失的关键经验：(1) 没有单一匹配算法在所有场景下都最优，建议对每个数据集训练多种算法族，并通过自动筛选确定最佳方案；(2) 精确率和召回率需分别优化，精确率依赖硬规则排除错误匹配，召回率则需增强候选集多样性；(3) 一个错误的匹配链接可能导致无关实体被隐性合并，因传递性假设会引发大规模误合并，因此跨组合并必须主动重新验证。这些教训旨在帮助从业者避免我们曾经历的数月无效实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体消歧研究缺乏对实际应用中复杂性和不确定性的充分考虑，导致实践中常因算法选择不当、精度与召回权衡失误及传递性误用而遭遇失败。作者希望通过实证研究揭示关键问题，为自服务系统设计提供实用指导。

Method: 在六个不同规模和特征的数据集上部署自服务实体消歧系统，采用多算法并行训练与自动性能比较（bake-off）机制，分析精确率与召回率的独立影响因素，并检验传递性假设在真实数据中的风险。

Result: 实验表明：单一算法无法通用；精确率与召回率需差异化优化策略；传递性假设在实际中易引发严重误合并，必须引入人工或验证机制进行干预。

Conclusion: 构建高效的自服务实体消歧系统需综合考虑算法多样性、精度与召回的分离优化以及跨组合并的主动验证机制。这三方面经验可显著提升系统鲁棒性与实用性，减少无效试错。

Abstract: We built and evaluated a self-serve entity resolution (ER) system on six benchmarks spanning 864 to 5M records, and three lessons emerged that are absent from existing ER literature. (1) No single matching algorithm wins everywhere - a self-serve pipeline cannot predict its next dataset, so we recommend training several algorithm families per dataset and letting an automatic bake-off pick the winner. (2) Precision and recall need separate fixes, not a shared threshold - precision needs hard rule-based vetoes, recall needs more diverse candidate retrieval. (3) One false-positive link can silently merge unrelated entities - assuming "A matches B" and "B matches C" implies "A matches C" lets a single bad link chain hundreds of records together, so every cross-group merge must be actively re-verified. We hope these lessons save practitioners the months of dead-end experiments that led us to them.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Implicit Causal World Models from Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26336)
*Jasorsi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐式因果世界模型，通过引入策略方差，利用顺序后门条件在不依赖预定义因果图的情况下从离线演示中恢复环境动态，在多智能体系统中有效应对分布变化，提升模型可解释性与干预强度下的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的强化学习中，世界模型常将统计相关性误认为因果机制，尤其在多智能体系统中，物理转移与智能体策略意图交织，导致模型在分布变化下失效。

Method: 引入策略方差以激活顺序后门条件，使世界模型能够从离线数据中识别因果结构，无需预先设定因果图。

Result: 在协调任务（双门、导航、让行）中，模型在完全与部分可观测场景下均表现出可解释的因果表示，模型精度随干预强度增加而提升。

Conclusion: 隐式因果世界模型有效提升了多智能体系统中环境动态建模的鲁棒性与可解释性，为应对分布外泛化提供了新路径。

Abstract: In model-based reinforcement learning, world models exist as internal simulators, but their training often conflates statistical correlations with causal mechanisms. This problem is exacerbated in multi-agent systems where physical transitions are intertwined with strategic agent intents, causing world models to fail under distribution shift. We introduce Implicit Causal World Models to recover environmental dynamics from offline demonstrations without requiring pre-defined causal graphs. By incorporating policy variance, we render world models discoverable via the sequential backdoor condition. Evaluations across coordination tasks (Two-Door, Navigation, and Giveway) demonstrate that these models provide interpretable causal representations under both full and partial observability, with model accuracy scaling directly with interventional strength.

</details>


### [150] [RAGuard: A Layered Defense Framework for Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems Against Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26339)
*Pushkal Kumar,Tucker Nielson,Tanish Kolhe,Shubham Zala,Vincent Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAGuard 是一种针对 RAG 系统中事实性语料库投毒攻击的分层防御机制。第一层通过对抗性微调密集检索器，使其在生成前降低恶意文档的排名；第二层 ZKIP（零知识推理补丁）是一种无需标签、黑盒的过滤器，通过留一法解码检测文档移除引起的语义和输出熵变化，从而识别并过滤有毒内容。ZKIP 在无须真实答案或模型内部信息的情况下，将攻击成功率降至 0.000，同时保持召回率接近干净语料基线。该方法适用于高比例投毒场景，但引入额外计算开销，可通过批处理与早停优化。此外，关键词保留型投毒对基于关键词的检索器影响较小，明确了威胁模型边界。代码与数据已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统依赖外部语料库，易受恶意注入的伪造事实、矛盾或推理陷阱等事实性投毒攻击影响，导致生成结果被误导。现有防御手段不足，亟需一种无需标注、不依赖模型内部结构且能有效识别复杂投毒行为的通用防御机制。

Method: 提出 RAGuard 分层防御体系：1）对抗性微调密集检索器，在合成投毒文档上训练以抑制恶意文档的召回；2）设计 ZKIP（Zero-Knowledge Inference Patch），基于留一法解码，通过分析单个文档移除后模型输出的语义偏移与熵变化，实现无监督、黑盒的毒性检测。利用模型自身在反事实上下文中的响应进行判断。

Result: 在 Natural Questions 数据集上，5%-30% 投毒比例下，仅使用对抗性微调仍无法完全消除攻击成功，而 ZKIP 将攻击成功率降至 0.000，同时召回率@5 仅下降 0.03，表现优异。在 BEIR (NFCorpus) 和 Natural Questions 上的监督分析验证了 ZKIP 所依赖的反事实信号可捕捉可学习的投毒模式。计算开销为每查询 $k+1$ 次生成，通过批处理与早停可有效缓解。关键词保留型投毒对 BM25 等词袋检索器影响极小，揭示了该防御的有效范围。

Conclusion: RAGuard 提供了一种高效、实用且无需依赖标签或模型内部信息的 RAG 安全增强方案，尤其在面对复杂事实性投毒攻击时表现出卓越鲁棒性。其核心思想——利用模型自身的反事实响应作为检测信号——具有普适潜力，为未来 LLM 安全系统设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems ground large language models (LLMs) in external corpora, but this reliance exposes them to corpus poisoning: maliciously injected passages that manipulate retrieved evidence. We introduce RAGuard, a layered defense against \emph{factual} corpus-poisoning attacks on RAG pipelines. The first layer adversarially fine-tunes a dense retriever on synthetic poisoned documents (fabricated facts, contradictions, and reasoning traps), teaching it to downrank malicious passages before generation. The second layer, the Zero-Knowledge Inference Patch ZKIP, is a label-free, black-box filter: for each retrieved document, it performs a leave-one-out decode and scores the document by the semantic shift and output-entropy change that its removal induces. ZKIP requires no poison labels, no ground-truth answers, and no access to model internals; it compares the model's own answers under counterfactual contexts. On poisoned Natural Questions at 5--30\% poison ratios, adversarial retriever training alone reduces but does not eliminate attack success, while ZKIP drives the measured attack success rate to 0.000 in every defended configuration, keeping Recall@5 within 0.03 of the clean-corpus baseline. Supervised analyses on both Natural Questions and BEIR (NFCorpus) confirm that the counterfactual signals ZKIP relies on carry learnable poison structure. The defense costs $k{+}1$ generator passes per query ($6\times$ for $k{=}5$); we analyze batching and early-stopping approximations that reduce this overhead. We also show that keyword-preserving poisons leave lexical retrievers such as BM25 essentially unaffected, an observation that delineates the boundary of the threat model. Code, datasets, and evaluation harnesses are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [151] [Automorphism-Induced Non-Canonicity in Top-k Explanations of Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26344)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Kaizhen Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了基于梯度的图神经网络解释器在处理具有化学对称性的分子（如两个等价硝基）时，因消息传递机制的置换等变性，导致无法区分对称部分，从而产生任意选择的问题。研究证明，当输入图存在非平凡自同构群且最小有效解释不被该群固定时，不存在同时满足单值性、最小性和对称性尊重的解释规则。作者提出一个无需参数的判别准则，通过 Lean 4 机械验证，可判断在特定稀疏预算下是否必须“切割”对称轨道。在 21298 个实例中，该准则与模型等价性检查完全一致，且无中立替代方案。对称性问题普遍存在：在 Mutagenicity 数据集中，93.4% 的分子存在非平凡自同构；在典型稀疏预算下，24.0% 的含两个等价硝基分子仅报告其中一个，结果完全任意。此外，模型本身也制造对称性——其无法区分化学上可区分的原子，而这种分辨能力取决于模型读取的信息而非参数化方式。报告对称轨道可消除任意性，仅需 0.11 毫秒和额外 0.43 条边/图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络解释方法在面对具有对称结构的分子（如两个等价硝基）时，会随机选择其中一个进行报告，造成解释结果的任意性。尽管这一现象常被归因于实现细节，但本文指出其本质是结构性障碍：由于消息传递机制天然具备置换等变性，任何输入图的自同构都会保持解释不变，因此无法区分对称部分。这挑战了标准解释报告（如 top-k 边）的合理性，尤其在实际应用中频繁出现此类对称情况。

Method: 提出一个形式化的判别准则，使用 Lean 4 进行机械化证明，无需公理依赖，仅根据图结构即可判断在给定稀疏预算下，是否所有最优解释都必须‘切割’对称轨道（即破坏对称性）。通过与机械模型等价性检查对比，验证该准则的准确性。同时分析真实数据集（Mutagenicity）中的对称性分布，并比较报告对称轨道与传统报告方式的性能差异。

Result: 在 21298 个实例中，所提准则与机械等价性检查结果完全一致，未发现例外；在典型稀疏预算下，24.0% 的含对称硝基分子仅报告一个，且该选择完全任意；93.4% 的 Mutagenicity 分子存在非平凡自同构；报告对称轨道可消除任意性，仅增加 0.11 毫秒延迟和 0.43 条额外边/图。

Conclusion: 解释方法的任意性并非实现缺陷，而是由图神经网络固有的对称性约束所决定的结构性障碍。在存在对称性的输入上，无法同时满足单值性、最小性和对称性尊重。建议采用报告对称轨道的方式以消除任意性，该方法高效且代价极低。

Abstract: A gradient-based GNN explainer given a molecule with two chemically equivalent nitro groups assigns them attribution scores that are equal to the last bit. It cannot do otherwise: message passing is exactly permutation equivariant, so any automorphism of the input leaves every attribution invariant. Yet the standard report, the top-k edges, names one of the two, and which one is settled by the order of an array. We show this is a structural obstruction rather than an implementation slip. When no minimal valid explanation is fixed by the input's automorphism group, no rule can be single-valued, minimal and symmetry-respecting at once. For the exact-k reports used in practice we give a parameter-free criterion, mechanised in Lean 4 with no axiom dependencies, that decides from the graph alone whether every score-optimal report of that size must split an orbit. Across 21298 instance-budget decisions the criterion agrees with a mechanical model-equivalence check without exception, and no severing case we found admitted a neutral alternative. The obstruction is common. Nontrivial automorphisms occur in 93.4% of Mutagenicity, the dataset the seminal explainability papers use, so the measure-zero dismissal of symmetric inputs, sound on the continuous domains it was made for, collapses here. At the sparsity budget those papers report, 24.0% of molecules with two interchangeable nitro groups (6 of 25) surface exactly one of them, every one arbitrary under mechanical verification. A model's blindness also manufactures symmetry: every MUTAG molecule contains atoms chemistry separates and the network provably cannot, and a matched control shows the resolution is set by what the model reads rather than how it is parameterised. Reporting orbits removes the arbitrariness at 0.11 ms and 0.43 extra edges per graph.

</details>


### [152] [MetaKoopman: Bayesian Meta-Learning of Koopman Operators for Modeling Structured Dynamics under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26345)
*Mahmoud Selim,Sriharsha Bhat,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaKoopman提出了一种基于贝叶斯元学习的框架，通过线性潜在表示建模非线性动态系统。它利用矩阵正态-逆威沙特（MNIW）先验对科普曼算子进行建模，实现基于近期轨迹片段的闭式贝叶斯更新，并提供未来状态轨迹的闭式后验预测分布，同时捕捉认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。在真实自动驾驶卡车-拖车系统和多种分布偏移的模拟控制任务中，该方法在多步预测精度、不确定性校准和对分布变化的鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。实地实验表明其在紧急避让和牵引极限操作等动态可行路径规划中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界系统中，非线性动态系统的建模与预测面临分布偏移带来的挑战，传统方法难以有效处理不确定性并保持鲁棒性。因此需要一种能够适应分布变化、同时量化不确定性并实现可靠决策的新型建模框架。

Method: 提出基于贝叶斯元学习的MetaKoopman框架，采用矩阵正态-逆威沙特（MNIW）先验对科普曼算子进行建模，支持从近期轨迹中进行闭式贝叶斯更新，并生成未来状态的后验预测分布，从而同时建模认知与偶然不确定性。

Result: 在真实自动驾驶卡车-拖车系统及多种分布偏移场景下，MetaKoopman在多步预测精度、不确定性校准和鲁棒性方面显著优于现有方法；实地实验验证了其在动态可行运动规划中的有效性，尤其在紧急避让和牵引极限操作中表现突出。

Conclusion: MetaKoopman通过贝叶斯元学习实现了对非线性动态系统的高效建模与不确定性量化，在面对分布偏移时展现出强鲁棒性，为复杂系统中的安全、可靠决策提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Modeling and forecasting nonlinear dynamics under distribution shifts is essential for robust decision-making in real-world systems. In this work, we propose MetaKoopman, a Bayesian meta-learning framework for modeling nonlinear dynamics through linear latent representations. MetaKoopman learns a Matrix Normal-Inverse Wishart (MNIW) prior over the Koopman operator, enabling closed-form Bayesian updates conditioned on recent trajectory segments. Moreover, it provides a closed-form posterior predictive distribution over future state trajectories, capturing both epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty in the learned dynamics. We evaluate MetaKoopman on a full-scale autonomous truck and trailer system across a wide range of adverse winter scenarios, including snow, ice, and mixed-friction conditions, as well as in simulated control tasks with diverse distribution shifts. MetaKoopman consistently outperforms prior approaches in multi-step prediction accuracy, uncertainty calibration, and robustness to distributional shifts. Field experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness in dynamically feasible motion planning, particularly during evasive maneuvers and operation at the limits of traction. Project website: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/MetaKoopman/

</details>


### [153] [High-Order Markov Blanket Discovery via a k-Order Relaxation of the Faithfulness Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26357)
*Loong Kuan Lee,Ragavi Krishnamoorthy,Nico Piatkowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种k阶松弛的忠实性假设，以捕捉k+2个变量之间的异或（XOR）和奇偶性类型关系。基于此，作者提出了一个名为kOMB的初步算法，用于在真实和经验性违反忠实性的情况下恢复变量的马尔可夫毯（MB）。实验表明，该方法在处理高阶依赖和有限样本中的伪依赖问题时具有有效性。代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数马尔可夫毯学习方法依赖于忠实性假设，即分布中的条件独立性对应图结构中的分离。然而，该假设容易被高阶依赖（如XOR、奇偶性关系）以及有限样本中的经验性偏差所破坏，导致错误的依赖推断。因此，需要一种更鲁棒的松弛假设来应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出了一种k阶放松的忠实性假设，允许表达k+2个变量间的奇偶性依赖关系；设计并实现了一个名为kOMB的算法，利用该假设进行马尔可夫毯发现，通过条件独立性测试与约束传播机制识别关键变量。

Result: 实验结果表明，kOMB能够在真实存在高阶依赖关系以及有限样本中出现伪依赖的情况下，准确恢复目标变量的马尔可夫毯，显著优于传统基于忠实性假设的方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的k阶忠实性松弛假设及相应的kOMB算法，为在违背传统忠实性假设的情形下实现鲁棒的马尔可夫毯学习提供了有效途径，具有在因果发现、特征选择和图模型构建等领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: The problem of learning the graphical Markov blanket (MB) of a variable from data has applications in many areas such as structure learning for Bayesian networks and Markov random fields, causal discovery, and feature selection. However, a common assumption most methods make is that the conditional independencies in the distribution imply the same separation in the graphical structure -- also known as the faithfulness assumption. Unfortunately, this assumption can be violated by higher-order dependencies such as XOR and parity-type relations, and -- on finite samples -- by empirical violations that, in extreme cases, even induce spurious dependencies absent from the true distribution. Therefore, in this paper we propose a "k-order" relaxation of the faithfulness assumption that captures parity type relationships between k+2 variables. We then propose a proof of concept algorithm called k-order Markov blanket (kOMB) that uses this relaxation for MB discovery. Finally, we empirically show how kOMB can recover the MB of a variable under both true and empirical violations of faithfulness. Code available at: https://github.com/lklee9/k-order-Markov-blanket

</details>


### [154] [Post-Training at the Edge of Detectability: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26358)
*Keegan Harris,Brian W. Lee,Ian Waudby-Smith,Philip Amortila,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于博弈论的框架，用于在强化学习微调中更合理地设置KL正则化系数。通过将智能体与监控者之间的序列博弈建模，该框架为奖励与参考策略保持之间的权衡提供了明确的统计解释，并推导出最优正则化参数，使得奖励每单位统计可区分性最大化。利用凸-凹分数规划的经典结果，该方法可简化为标准的KL正则化强化学习目标，便于集成到现有训练流程中。实验表明，该方法在持续学习场景下实现了良好的奖励保留平衡，并可用于审计开源模型的API服务提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习微调中，KL正则化系数通常通过启发式或超参数搜索设定，缺乏理论依据，导致训练成本高或性能不佳。需要一种更系统、可解释的方法来确定该系数。

Method: 构建一个序列博弈模型，其中智能体最大化累积奖励，而监控者检测策略输出是否偏离参考策略。通过分析该博弈的均衡解，发现其等价于一个带有最优正则化参数的KL正则化强化学习问题，且该参数可通过凸-凹分数规划方法学习。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B上的实验表明，该方法在持续学习任务中实现了与现有方法相当甚至更优的奖励-保留权衡；同时，该框架可用于评估和审计开放模型的API服务。

Conclusion: 所提出的博弈论框架为强化学习微调中的正则化系数选择提供了理论基础，使参数学习更加自动化和可解释，且能无缝集成至现有训练流程，提升了模型微调的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning is widely used in language model training to improve model performance on a target task while limiting drift from a reference policy. A standard way to balance this trade-off is via a KL-regularized RL objective, although this formulation does not by itself provide a principled way to set the regularization coefficient. In practice, the coefficient is typically chosen heuristically or via hyperparameter search, which can lead to unnecessary overhead in training cost or undesirable reward-retention trade-offs. We instead propose a game-theoretic framework that gives this trade-off an explicit statistical interpretation. Specifically, we study a sequential game in which an agent chooses a policy to maximize cumulative reward while a monitor observes policy outputs over time and tests for deviations from the reference policy. Although not originating from the same perspective, we show that the resulting equilibrium policy can nonetheless be expressed as the solution to a KL-regularized RL problem for an optimal regularization parameter that can be viewed as maximizing reward per unit of statistical distinguishability. Drawing on classical results from concave-convex fractional programming, we provide a principled method for learning this equilibrium coefficient via reduction to the KL-regularized RL objective, thus allowing for flexible integration into standard fine-tuning pipelines. In experiments with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.2-1B, we demonstrate that our methods result in competitive reward-retention trade-offs in a continual learning setting, and illustrate how our framework may be used to audit API providers serving open-source models.

</details>


### [155] [ClockRoPE: Random Fourier Rotations for Temporal Routine Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26369)
*Yiwen Chen,Joshua Ainslie,Krzysztof Choromanski,Xiang Gao,Su-Lin Wu,Yiping Yuan,Qian Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClockRoPE，一种基于周期性注意力调制函数的旋转位置编码方法，用于改进序列推荐中的长期依赖建模。通过理论分析发现，任意正定注意力调制函数可通过其傅里叶变换诱导的随机旋转近似，从而实现更灵活的频率设计。实验表明，ClockRoPE在真实场景中显著提升用户参与度指标，并已成功部署于主流视频平台的生成式检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RoPE采用对数线性频率调度，难以捕捉序列推荐中复杂的周期性依赖关系，限制了其在具有复杂距离相关性的场景中的应用。

Method: 提出随机傅里叶旋转理论，证明任何正定注意力调制函数可由其傅里叶变换诱导的随机旋转近似；基于此构建ClockRoPE，利用周期性调制函数生成旋转频率，以建模序列中的规律性模式。

Result: 在线A/B测试显示，ClockRoPE在关键用户参与度指标上持续提升，已在大规模生成式检索系统中成功落地应用。

Conclusion: ClockRoPE通过引入周期性旋转频率，有效提升了模型对序列中规律性模式的建模能力，在实际推荐系统中展现出显著性能优势。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) has been widely adopted in transformer-based large language models. However, its log-linear frequency schedule, originally designed to produce long-term attention decay, limits its adoption in domains with more complex distance-correlation patterns, such as temporal periodicity in sequential recommendation. We investigate the expressiveness of general query/key rotations and find that any normalized continuous positive-definite attention modulation function can be approximated by random rotations induced by its own Fourier transform, which we term Random Fourier Rotations. Building on this theory, we propose ClockRoPE for routine modeling in sequential recommendation, where rotation frequencies are derived from periodic attention modulation functions. In online A/B tests, ClockRoPE demonstrates consistent improvements in valued engagement metrics, and has been successfully deployed in production-scale generative retrieval system at a major video-sharing platform.

</details>


### [156] [Q-Steer: Action-Value Guidance for Molecular Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26391)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Steer是一种用于分子语言模型的滚动时动作价值引导方法，通过使用离线训练的固定前缀-动作价值评分器PAVS-Q，在生成过程中为每个候选下一个标记添加归一化的价值奖励，以改善分子优化的奖励信号。该方法在不改变在线查询预算的前提下，显著提升了多种分子生成模型和优化器组合下的性能，验证了其通用性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子优化因延迟反馈导致策略优化短视，无法明确哪些中间步骤贡献了最终奖励；因此需要一种机制在生成过程中提供更及时、更精确的动作价值反馈，以指导更好的决策。

Method: 引入Q-Steer框架，利用一个预先训练并冻结的前缀-动作价值评分器PAVS-Q，估算在给定部分SMILES前缀下采取某个候选动作的下游奖励，并将归一化后的价值奖励加到采样逻辑中，从而在不改变原有优化器更新规则和在线查询预算的情况下提升生成质量。

Result: 在PMO23基准上，固定10,000次在线调用预算下，所有8个骨干-优化器组合均实现有效唯一性得分提升，平均得分增益为+0.033至+0.049，每组任务中有18-20次表现更优；机制实验表明动作身份至关重要，广播值几乎无影响，而打乱动作值会损害性能。

Conclusion: Q-Steer作为一种可复用的滚动时动作价值包装器，能够在不增加计算成本或改变在线预算的前提下，普遍提升不同优化器家族和策略骨干的分子优化平均奖励，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Oracle-limited molecular optimization gives reward only after a complete molecule is generated, while each rollout requires many local next-token decisions. This delayed-feedback interface makes molecular policy optimization myopic: an optimizer can learn that a molecule was good without knowing which intermediate actions made it good. We introduce Q-Steer, a rollout-time action-value steering primitive for molecular language models. Q-Steer uses an offline-trained and frozen prefix-action value scorer, PAVS-Q, that estimates the downstream reward of taking a candidate next token under a partial SMILES prefix, then adds a normalized value bonus to sampling logits. The optimizer update rule and online oracle budget are unchanged; the claim is fixed-online-oracle performance, not equal total compute. On PMO23 with a fixed 10,000-call online budget, complete factorial studies across two molecular language-model backbones and four optimizers show that Q-Steer improves mean valid-unique score in all eight backbone-optimizer cells, with positive macro mean-score gains between +0.033 and +0.049 and 18-20 task wins per cell. Mechanism controls show that action identity matters: prefix-broadcast values are nearly neutral, while shuffled action values harm performance. These results support Q-Steer as a reusable rollout-time action-value wrapper that improves average molecular optimization reward across optimizer families and policy backbones without changing the online oracle budget.

</details>


### [157] [Flow Map Learning via Nongradient Vector Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26398)
*Mark Goldstein,Anshuk Uppal,Raghav Singhal,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGFlow 是一种学习流映射的新方法，通过非保守动力学在无需显式可逆性约束和昂贵的迭代反向传播的情况下，直接从头训练模型以计算 ODE 解和隐含速度。它在 CIFAR 基准上于 10 步采样时取得最佳 FID，且在其他步数下仍具竞争力，并唯一具备基于 stopgrad 动力学的稳定点保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流模型在推理时因采样需积分而存在显著开销；一致性模型虽缓解此问题，但面临需要模型逆或迭代反向传播等计算挑战，且未证明所求流映射是损失函数的解。

Method: SGFlow 采用非保守动力学，通过设置一个目标流映射为稳定点，训练模型从头计算 ODE 解和隐含速度，避免显式可逆性要求和迭代反向传播。

Result: 在 CIFAR 基准上，SGFlow 在 10 步采样时达到最优 FID，其他步数下与流匹配、均值流、拉格朗日映射匹配方法相当，且唯一具备停止梯度动力学的稳定点理论保证。

Conclusion: SGFlow 提供了一种高效且理论上可靠的流映射学习框架，在保持高生成质量的同时显著降低推理复杂度，为一致性建模开辟新路径。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models benefit from simple regression losses, but inference incurs significant overhead because sampling requires integration. Consistency models address this by directly learning the flow maps along the ODE trajectory, opening a design space between one-step and many-step approaches. However, existing methods face computational challenges such as requiring model inverses or backpropagation through iterated model calls, and do not always prove that the desired ODE flow map is a solution to the loss. We introduce SGFlow, an approach for learning flow maps that bypasses explicit invertibility constraints and expensive differentiation through model iteration. SGFlow trains a model to compute both the ODE solutions and the implied velocity from scratch by following non-conservative dynamics with a stationary point at the desired flow map. On the CIFAR image benchmark, no single method attains the best FID at every step count: SGFlow attains the best FID at 10 sampling steps and remains competitive with flow matching, Meanflow, and Lagrangian map matching at other step counts, while being the only one with a proven stationary-point guarantee for its stopgrad-based dynamics.

</details>


### [158] [Examining the Efficacy of Graph Neural Network Message-Passing in Regression Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26404)
*Keith G. Mills,Aedan J. DeFrates,Joong Ho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了图神经网络（GNN）在回归任务中的表现，发现深度卷积型GNN（如GEN）在排序、误差最小化和洞察提取等回归任务中优于基于注意力的GNN，而一些经典且理论基础扎实的GNN仍具有竞争力和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在分类任务中广泛评估，但其在回归任务中的表现常被忽视，而现有最佳分类GNN常被直接用于回归任务，缺乏针对性优化，因此需要系统评估GNN在多种回归场景下的有效性。

Method: 通过在多种回归任务（如排名、误差最小化、洞察提取）上测试不同类型的GNN层，包括深度卷积型GNN（如GEN）、注意力型GNN以及经典理论型GNN，进行对比分析。

Result: 深度卷积型GNN在多数回归任务中表现更优，尤其在复杂建模能力方面；注意力型GNN表现相对较弱；经典理论型GNN则保持良好的效率与竞争力。

Conclusion: GNN的设计应根据具体任务类型进行优化，当前以分类为导向的评估范式不足以全面反映GNN的能力，回归任务需专门设计和评估更适合的GNN架构。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNN) facilitate effective prediction on graph data such as molecules, media networks and neural network blueprints. GNNs facilitate prediction through message passing techniques which define how information flows from a node to its neighbors. Due to the ubiquity of the graph data type, the development of newer and better GNNs has garnered much interest in the machine learning community.
  However, GNN evaluation and benchmarking is primarily driven by classification tasks. Thus, prospective GNN message passing layers are evaluated on their ability to outperform prior work in classification contexts. In contrast, GNNs are equally capable of performing scalar regression prediction, yet this class of problem is often overlooked when proposing new GNNs while the best classification GNNs are utilized in an a priori or off-the-shelf manner for regression problems. In response, this paper studies the efficacy of GNN layers in a slew of regression contexts from rank ordering, error minimization and insight extraction. Results show that deep convolutional GNNs, particularly GEN, are more effective at these tasks than attention-based GNNs, while other classical, theoretically-inspired GNNs remain competitive and efficient.

</details>


### [159] [SCOUT: Per-Context Reset Curricula for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26417)
*Siddharth Aphale,Ayushman Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOUT 是一种无需依赖学习器的在线重置控制器，为每个任务上下文（context）提供个性化课程。它通过二元成功信号动态调整辅助（scaffold）的使用：在持续成功后逐步移除辅助，失败后恢复辅助，并在进展停滞时谨慎尝试更难的起始状态。该方法不改变奖励、优化器或学习算法。实验表明，相较于全局统一的移除节奏，SCOUT 在多任务导航与操作场景中显著提升学习效率，尤其在原始方法无法完成的任务中实现突破。在存在上下文间学习速率冲突的构造案例中，所有全局调度均导致某一组任务失败，而 SCOUT 能同时解决两组问题。此外，传统分组级调度依赖已知分组标签，而 SCOUT 无需标签，在组内差异和跨组差异场景下均表现稳定。核心结论是：重置课程应按学习进度差异的尺度来调节辅助移除时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励强化学习中，由于初始随机探索难以到达任务后期阶段，训练常失败。现有基于重置的课程（reset curriculum）通过引入中间状态（scaffold）作为辅助起点改善这一问题，但其普遍采用全局统一的辅助移除节奏，无法适应不同任务实例或上下文的学习速度差异，导致部分上下文过度辅助或过早去辅助，影响整体性能。

Method: 提出 SCOUT——一个无需修改学习器的在线、自适应重置控制器。它基于二元成功信号（成功/失败）进行决策：持续成功则缓慢减少辅助；失败则立即恢复辅助；若连续多次尝试未见进展，则试探更难的起始点。整个过程由计数机制驱动，无需显式建模环境或策略，且可适用于任意学习算法。

Result: 在六个导航与操作任务中，使用 scaffold 的训练显著提升成功率，其中三个原本无法在预算内完成的任务得以解决。在构造的‘节奏冲突’场景中，所有全局调度策略均使一组任务失败，而 SCOUT 成功解决全部任务。此外，平均成功率可能掩盖组别间的失败，因此报告最差组的表现也显示 SCOUT 优势。即使在无分组标签的情况下，SCOUT 仍保持一致性能，优于依赖分组的策略。

Conclusion: 重置课程应根据学习进度差异的尺度动态调整辅助移除节奏。全局同步的节奏可能因上下文学习速率不一致而失效，而 SCOUT 通过为每个上下文独立调控课程，实现了更强鲁棒性与泛化能力，是解决复杂任务中学习异质性的有效方案。

Abstract: Sparse-reward reinforcement learning often fails because rollouts from the unassisted evaluation start rarely reach later task stages. Reset curricula address this by starting some training rollouts from easier intermediate states, called scaffolds. Such a curriculum faces two decisions: scaffold access, obtaining informative starts, and scaffold allocation, deciding how quickly that assistance is removed. Most prior curricula pace removal on one shared schedule, which can fail when task instances, or contexts, learn at different rates. We introduce SCOUT, an online, learner-agnostic reset controller that gives every context its own curriculum. Using only binary rollout success, SCOUT removes assistance after sustained success, restores it after failure, and cautiously tests a harder start when progress stalls, without changing the reward, optimizer, or learner. A counting construction shows that synchronized global pacing can be insufficient when contexts need conflicting amounts of assisted practice. Across six navigation and manipulation settings, scaffold access improves learning and enables success in three where unassisted training fails within the reported budget. In a constructed pacing conflict, each tested global schedule leaves one group unsolved, while SCOUT solves both. Average success can conceal this failure, so we also report the least successful group. Group-level pacing works when learning differences follow known groups but can fail when they occur within one group. SCOUT needs no group labels and remains consistently strong in both cases. A reset curriculum should remove assistance at the scale where learning progress differs.

</details>


### [160] [Existence-Field Diffusion Model for Spatial Point Processes with Variable Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26428)
*Xiaoyin Pan,Christian R. Shelton,Rakshith Mahishi,Chengkuan Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出存在场扩散模型（EFDM）用于空间点过程的生成建模，通过为每个潜在点分配存在变量来统一建模点的位置与数量，避免了离散的变维操作，实现了灵活且对称的生成动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在处理可变基数的空间点过程时面临挑战，因为它们通常将基数和空间结构分开建模，或依赖于离散的变维操作，导致生成过程不灵活且不对称。

Method: 提出存在场扩散模型（EFDM），引入存在变量表示每个潜在点的出现程度，从而在统一的扩散过程中联合建模空间位置与点的数量，无需显式离散转换。

Result: 在具有不同基数的数据集上，所提方法表现出更强的建模能力，验证了其灵活性与通用性。

Conclusion: EFDM提供了一个统一、灵活且高效的框架，用于空间点过程的生成建模，显著提升了对复杂分布的建模性能。

Abstract: We study generative modeling of spatial point processes (SPP), where both the number of points and their spatial configuration are governed by a joint distribution. While diffusion models have achieved strong performance in modeling complex distributions, extending them to variable-cardinality SPP remains challenging. Existing approaches either decouple the modeling of cardinality and spatial structure, or rely on discrete trans-dimensional operations to modify the number of points, resulting in inflexible and asymmetric generative dynamics. We propose the existence-field diffusion model (EFDM) for spatial point processes modeling, where each potential point is associated with an existence variable representing its degree of presence. This enables a unified diffusion process that jointly models both spatial locations and cardinality without requiring explicit discrete transitions. We demonstrate that our approach provides a flexible and general framework for generative modeling of spatial point processes, achieving improved modeling capability on datasets with varying cardinality.

</details>


### [161] [DHRCL:Training Code LLMs with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26457)
*Shuhang Wang,Ziming Li,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DHRCL 是一种基于密集分层奖励和课程学习的强化学习框架，用于代码生成模型的后训练。它将反馈分解为语法验证、执行成功、单元测试通过率和基于抽象语法树（AST）的结构相似性，并通过三阶段课程（语法、执行、通过率与结构）自动调节训练过程。引入阶段感知的概率性标记信用再分配机制，遵循‘巩固到优化’原则，在不同阶段动态调整奖励分配策略。在 Qwen3-8B 和 KodCode 协议下实验表明，DHRCL 在多种基准方法中表现更优，且随着模型规模增大（4B、8B、14B），优势依然稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成的强化学习方法依赖稀疏结果奖励或静态组合异构密集信号，无法有效区分和逐步提升语法正确性、可执行性、功能正确性和结构组织等逐级递进的编程能力。因此需要一种能解耦并分阶段优化这些能力的框架。

Method: 提出 DHRCL 框架，包含：(1) 四重密集奖励信号：语法有效性、执行成功、单元测试通过率、AST 结构相似性；(2) 三阶段自适应课程学习：语法、执行、通过率与结构，阶段切换由近期验证趋势自动决定；(3) 阶段感知的令牌信用再分配机制：根据阶段目标动态调整奖励传播方式——语法阶段强调已建立模式，执行阶段均匀传播反馈，功能优化阶段对未成熟决策施加更大奖惩。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 和 KodCode 基准上，DHRCL 显著优于二值奖励、通过率奖励、基于奖励模型及可验证密集奖励基线。在 Qwen3-4B、8B、14B 多个模型规模下均保持性能优势，证明其可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DHRCL 通过分层奖励与自适应课程学习，实现了对代码生成中多维度编程能力的渐进式优化。阶段感知的信用再分配机制有效提升了训练效率与模型泛化能力，是代码导向大模型后训练的有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural post-training paradigm for code-oriented large language models because generated programs can be evaluated through parsing, execution, unit tests, and structural analysis.However, existing methods often rely on sparse outcome rewards or statically combine heterogeneous dense signals, even though syntax validity, executability, functional correctness, and structural organization describe different and progressively dependent programming capabilities. We propose DHRCL, a reinforcement learning framework with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning. DHRCL decomposes feedback into syntax validation, execution success, unit-test pass rate, and AST-based structural similarity, and organizes these signals through a three-stage Syntax, Execution, Pass & Structural curriculum. Stage duration is determined automatically from recent validation trends rather than manually specified capability thresholds. We further introduce stage-aware probability-based token credit redistribution. The mechanism follows a consolidation-to-refinement principle: it emphasizes established token patterns during syntax-oriented optimization, applies uniform propagation for non-local execution feedback, and allocates more credit or blame to less-established token decisions during final functional optimization. Under a unified Qwen3-8B and KodCode protocol, the experiments compare DHRCL with binary, pass-rate, reward-model-based, and verifiable dense-reward baselines. We further evaluate DHRCL across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B backbones, showing that its advantage remains consistent as model capacity increases.

</details>


### [162] [Neural Architecture Search for Traffic Prediction: A Survey of Methods, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26467)
*Truong Giang Vu,Li Yang,Richard W. Pazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了用于交通预测的神经架构搜索（NAS）方法，按搜索策略分为基于梯度、进化和单次权重共享三类。分析了各类方法如何设计搜索空间以覆盖时空交通算子，并探讨其在成本与模型质量之间的权衡。还讨论了计算可扩展性、手动设计搜索空间、跨城市泛化、动态图结构及时空基础模型的NAS等开放挑战，指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习模型在交通预测中依赖人工设计架构，耗时且泛化能力差。NAS提供自动化架构搜索，能更好地匹配交通数据的时空结构，减少人工干预，提升模型性能与泛化性。

Method: 根据搜索策略分类：基于梯度的方法、进化算法、单次权重共享方法。每类方法分析其搜索空间设计（涵盖空间与时间算子）及搜索策略对计算成本与模型质量的平衡机制。

Result: 总结了现有NAS方法在交通预测中的有效性，揭示了不同方法在效率与性能上的优劣，指出当前在大规模路网、跨城市迁移、动态图建模等方面仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: NAS为交通预测提供了高效自动化的模型设计路径，但需进一步解决计算成本、通用性与动态建模问题；未来应探索面向时空基础模型的NAS框架，推动智能交通系统智能化发展。

Abstract: Traffic prediction is a core task in intelligent transportation systems, supporting applications such as adaptive signal control, route guidance, and ride-hailing dispatch. Deep learning models, including graph convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and Transformers, achieve strong results on standard benchmarks, but their architectures are designed by hand, requiring significant expert effort and producing models that often generalize poorly across cities and datasets. Neural Architecture Search (NAS) offers a systematic alternative to manual design. It automates the search over candidate architectures of deep learning models, finding designs that match the spatial-temporal structure of traffic data without manual trial and error. This survey reviews NAS methods applied to traffic prediction, organized by search strategy: gradient-based methods, evolutionary methods, and one-shot weight-sharing methods. For each category, we analyze how the search space is designed to cover spatial and temporal traffic operators, and how the search strategy balances cost against architecture quality. We also discuss open challenges, computational scalability to large road networks, manual search space design, cross-city generalization, dynamic graph structure, and the open question of NAS for spatial-temporal foundation models, and identify directions for future research.

</details>


### [163] [Learning Dynamic User Personas from Implicit Interaction Streams via Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26473)
*Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS 是一种从隐式交互流中学习动态用户画像的框架，通过提取日常对话中的行为信号并利用预测驱动的闭环迭代优化画像表示，无需显式反馈。在合成数据和真实 Reddit 数据上的实验表明，IRIS 能生成稳定且区分度高的用户画像，在决策预测任务中表现最佳（准确率 61.0%），优于静态画像、仅依赖记忆检索等方法，证明隐式行为建模是个性化大模型的可扩展替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化大语言模型的方法通常依赖显式偏好监督（如成对比较或人口统计信息），难以适用于自然交互场景。因此需要一种无需显式反馈、能从日常互动中自动构建用户画像的新方法。

Method: IRIS 框架通过分析用户的历史交互流，提取隐式行为信号，并基于行为预测任务构建闭环反馈机制，持续迭代优化用户画像表示，实现动态个性化。

Result: 在合成数据上，IRIS 能生成稳定且具有区分性的用户画像；在真实 Reddit AITA 数据集上，其决策预测准确率达到 61.0%，显著优于静态画像、记忆检索及无个性化基线方法。

Conclusion: 隐式行为建模为个性化大语言模型提供了一种可扩展的替代路径，有助于构建能够持续演化的自适应对话系统与具身智能体。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) to individual users is essential for improving user experience, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit preference supervision such as pairwise comparisons or demographic attributes, limiting their applicability in natural interaction settings. We propose IRIS, a framework that learns dynamic user personas directly from implicit interaction streams by extracting behavioral signals from everyday conversations and iteratively refining persona representations through a prediction-driven closed loop without requiring explicit feedback. We introduce an evaluation protocol based on behavior prediction, persona stability, and decision prediction. A proof-of-concept study on a synthetic interaction stream derived from public-domain autobiographical text shows that IRIS produces stable personas and distinguishes individual users while revealing limitations of memory-only approaches on recall-oriented metrics. We then validate IRIS on anonymized real-world Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA) data, with personas built solely from each author's historical interactions.
